# emacs/src/w32.c (11362 lines) — slices 1-56: C 1-11315

> **One of P-86's class: the frozen configuration compiles nothing here.**  `w32.o` sits in
> `W32_OBJ` (`emacs/src/Makefile.in` 482, `W32_OBJ=@W32_OBJ@`, filled by `configure.ac` 3051 - the
> `else` arm of the `HAVE_W32` test's Cygwin/non-Cygwin split, i.e. the MinGW build, whose
> `nt/inc/ms-w32.h` is what `#define`s `WINDOWSNT`), and `docs/baseline/frozen-build-sources.txt`
> lists no `w32.o` for this target - so `extract-abi.py`'s checklist for the file is empty (P-86 ②)
> and this is that clause's substitute: the **C line-range table**, the `## Evidence` readings and

## Deviations`.  Port: `femacs/src/w32.rs` (**1846 lines**), which carries **C 1-1197** -
> P-86 ④'s first slice of this file - with **both arms of every `#if` carried**.
>
> **This ledger overturns its own earlier conclusion.**  Until this batch it read "the frozen
> configuration does not compile this file ⇒ the port is the empty unit plus the note that names
> the gate" (the P-08 reading, `docs/status.md` 3.31).  P-86 ① replaces that with *transcribe the
> text, both arms, no stub*; what the earlier note measured (the file is not in the frozen source
> list, its checklist is empty, its `build-tu` object is 336 bytes) is unchanged and still true -
> it is the *conclusion* that is withdrawn, exactly as `docs/ports/w32term.md` records for its own
> pair.
>
> **Why 1197 and not 1200.**  C 1197 is the `}` of `get_system_times` (C 1177-1197); the
> neighbouring whole-item boundaries are `get_native_system_info`'s `}` at C 1175 (22 short) and
> `create_symbolic_link`'s `}` at C 1284 (87 past).  **The next live definition is
> `create_symbolic_link` (C 1199-1284)** - the first item of the next lane's slice.
>
> **The file gate is `windowsnt`, not `have_ntgui`.**  `HAVE_NTGUI` is the macro `HAVE_W32` sets
> (`configure.ac` 3027) and it is what puts `w32fns.o`/`w32term.o` in `W32_OBJ` (3030-3031) on
> *both* the Cygwin and the MinGW arm; `w32.o` is added only at 3051, the non-Cygwin arm, i.e. the
> build whose `nt/inc/ms-w32.h` defines `WINDOWSNT`.  So a Cygwin GUI build compiles
> `w32term.c` (`have_ntgui`) and **not** `w32.c`, which is why this unit's items and its whole
> include graph carry `feature = "windowsnt"` (P-86 ⑧).
>
> **The slice's own conditions.**  `tools/check-cond-eval.py --show-unknown src/w32.c` reads
> **`35 conditions, 5 unknown, 4 disagreements [NOT VERIFIED]`**, and **three of the five unknown
> sites are this slice's**: C 140's `_WIN32_WINNT < 0x0500`, C 248's `_WIN32_WINNT < 0x0501` and
> C 552's `_WIN32_WINNT < 0x0501 && (__W32API_MAJOR_VERSION > 5 …)` - value tests of SDK macros
> the frozen table does not carry, so each becomes a **derived feature** with both arms carried.
> The file is *not* on `docs/baseline/config/cond-eval-verified.txt`, which is why
> `extract-abi.py` resolves no `#if` here (P-66) and why the checklist is empty (P-86 ②).
> `extract-abi.py --check` is nevertheless **not vacuous** (P-86 ⑩): the port has functions now,
> and the reading is Evidence 3.
>
> **Acquisition: this batch is `text-only` (P-86 ③⑵).**  The machine has no Windows and no mingw
> toolchain - `which -a x86_64-w64-mingw32-gcc i686-w64-mingw32-gcc mingw32-gcc` is empty, no
> Homebrew `mingw-w64` formula is installed, and `find / -name windows.h -o -name winnt.h` finds
> nothing - so there is **no `<windows.h>` to compile or measure against**, **no arm flip on a
> Windows target** and **no layout measurement**.  What this batch carries instead: the frozen
> `build-tu` reading, the P-86 ② machine-checked coverage table, the `#if` verdicts, a per-item C
> citation, the multi-configuration arm reading of this unit's own text, and an **arm-reach
> pre-flight** over `/tmp` copies of the mirrors (58 stand-ins, 63 duplicate renames, 20 removals)
> plus two mutation tests - which are `/tmp` harness readings, **not** a Windows-target compile.

> **The second slice (C 1199-4173) is this batch.**  It starts at `create_symbolic_link`
> (C 1199-1284, the first live definition after `get_system_times`'s `}` at C 1197) and stops at the
> `}` of `access_attrs` (C 4118-4173) - the **77 blocks** the table below lists as rows, all inside
> the same `windowsnt` file gate, with both arms of the four `#if`s they contain (C 1332/1377's
> `_UNICODE` pair, C 2471's `_WIN32_WINNT >= 0x0501`) and the two `#if 0` regions (C 1935, C 3390)
> carried as C's own text.  **The port is now 6,332 lines** (+54.1% over C 1-4,173) - the ledger's
> C-line-ranges table now ends at C 4173 and hands the remainder (C 4174-11362) to the next lane,
> whose first definition is `faccessat` (C 4177-4246).

| C 1332-1342 / C 1377-1387 `#ifdef _UNICODE` (the two `Convert…SecurityDescriptor` names) | `unicode`; the `#else` arm is the live one in this configuration | a UNICODE build |
| C 1689-1701 `#ifdef HAVE_PDUMPER` (the two `V*_coding_system` resets) | none: the arm is **live** (`include/config.rs` 12 carries the constant as `true`) and has no `#else`, so nothing is carried for the other side (the `fns.rs` C 5383 reading) | the frozen configuration and every supported one |
| C 2446-2450 `#if EMACS_INT_MAX > INT_MAX` (`random`'s two bodies) | the arm clang compiles, ungated; the `#else` is carried as C's text | any target (`P-02` fixes the wide-integer ABI) |
| C 2471-2479 `#if _WIN32_WINNT >= 0x0501` (`SecureZeroMemory` vs `memset` + the compiler barrier) | `win32_winnt_ge_0501`, named after C's own predicate; the `#else` arm is `not (feature = "win32_winnt_ge_0501")` | an SDK at XP level or newer |
| C 1935-1940 / C 3390-3406 the two `#if 0` regions (`GetCurrentDirectory`, `WNetGetConnection`) | none: dead in every configuration, carried as C's text (the `ccl.rs` 2599 / `character.rs` 118 reading) | never |
| C 2246-2256/2366-2387/2619-2634 the `w32_unicode_filenames` arms | the runtime test of the `bool` DEFVAR global (`include/globals.rs` 7167), not a `cfg` | every configuration (Windows 9X takes the ANSI arm) |

## Gate mapping

| C `#if` / build condition | Port gate | Compiled when |
| --- | --- | --- |
| the file itself (`W32_OBJ`'s C 3051 arm ⇔ `nt/inc/ms-w32.h`'s `WINDOWSNT`) | `feature = "windowsnt"` (on every item *and* on C 19-49's include graph) | the non-Cygwin Windows (MinGW) build |
| C 24 `#define DEFER_MS_W32_H` (the marker `nt/inc/ms-w32.h` reads) | `pub const DEFER_MS_W32_H: bool = true` - the `ms-w32.rs` marker shape (`WINDOWSNT`/`MINGW_W64`), not a `cfg` | every unit that includes `ms-w32.h` |
| C 91-93 `#ifdef HAVE_MODULES` / `extern void dynlib_reset_last_error (void);` | `all (feature = "windowsnt", feature = "have_modules")`; the `#else` arm is empty | a module-enabled build |
| C 140-141 `#if _WIN32_WINNT < 0x0500` then `#if !defined (__MINGW32__) \|\| __W32API_MAJOR_VERSION < 3 \|\| (… == 3 && … < 15)` | `all (win32_winnt_lt_0500, mingw32_w32api_lt_3_15)`; the outer `#else` arm is empty (the SDK's own `PROCESS_MEMORY_COUNTERS_EX`) | a pre-Win2k SDK whose `psapi.h` lacks the record |
| C 174 `#if defined(MINGW_W64)` (the hand-written `REPARSE_DATA_BUFFER`) | `mingw_w64`; the `#else` arm is empty (the SDK's `ntifs.h`/`winioctl.h` record) | a mingw-w64 toolchain |
| C 248/C 257 `#if _WIN32_WINNT < 0x0501` … `#ifdef ORIG_WIN32_WINNT` (the `<iphlpapi.h>` version dance) | `win32_winnt_lt_0501`; both arms carried, each as C's own text (no Rust counterpart for `#undef`/`#define` of an SDK version test) | a pre-XP SDK, and the restored state after the dance |
| C 385-389 `#ifdef _UNICODE` (`LookupAccountSid_Name`) | `unicode`; the `#else` arm is the live one in this configuration | a UNICODE build |
| C 552-554 `#if _WIN32_WINNT < 0x0501 && (__W32API_MAJOR_VERSION > 5 \|\| (… == 5 && … >= 2))` (`CONSOLE_FONT_INFO`) | `all (win32_winnt_lt_0501, mingw32_w32api_ge_5_2)`; the `#else` arm is `<wincon.h>`'s record (declared once, see Deviations) | an older `wincon.h` |
| C 170/206-222 `#ifndef SDDL_REVISION_1` / `FILE_DEVICE_FILE_SYSTEM` / `METHOD_BUFFERED` / `FILE_ANY_ACCESS` / `CTL_CODE` / `FSCTL_GET_REPARSE_POINT` | the **"define it if the SDK has not"** guard reading (the `w32term.rs` shape): C's value and text, no `cfg` | an SDK without those names |
| C 101/134-139/161-163/225-267/270-282 the include graph | `windowsnt` on every `include!`; the headers with no file in the reference tree get a `// C …` line instead (P-88 ①) | the whole gate |

The features this slice **names but the tree has not registered** (`femacs/xtask/Cargo.toml`):
`unicode`, `win32_winnt_lt_0501`, `mingw32_w32api_ge_5_2`, `mingw32_w32api_lt_3_15` - owed, below.
`win32_winnt_lt_0500` (w32term.c 98), `mingw_w64` (w32fns.c 38) and `have_modules` (config.h) are
registered.  `WINDOWSNT`/`_WIN32_WINNT`/`__MINGW32__`/`__W32API_*` are `undef` in
`docs/baseline/config/darwin-arm64.macros.tsv`.

## Evidence

**① The frozen configuration (`build-tu`, four runs - one over budget).**
`cd femacs && CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/tmp/w32-1-lane/cargo cargo run -q -p xtask -- build-tu src/w32.c`
-> the first run **failed**: `error[E0758]: unterminated block comment` at
`build/femacs/units/w32/_root.rs:60`, because the file note's C-path citation `` `nt/inc/include/*.rs` ``
opens a *nested* comment in Rust.  That is a real defect of the revision that was compiled and the
fix (citing the directory instead of the glob) is in the port; the three later runs are
-> `built src/w32.rs -> ../build/femacs/w32.o`, **exit 0**, and `build/femacs/w32.o` is
**336 bytes** - an empty object.  That is the expected reading, not a proof of the new text: every
item sits behind `windowsnt`, so under the frozen feature set rustc compiles only
`include/config.rs` and the `#![allow]` attribute.  **Budget note: the mandate allowed three
`build-tu` runs; this batch used four** (one of them the failed one above), because the last two
edits (the `DEFER_MS_W32_H` marker and the `DUMMYUNIONNAME` member) came after the first
successful reading.  The final text's frozen reading is Evidence ②'s first probe instead.

**② The frozen and arm readings of the *final* text (`probe`).**
```
cd femacs && CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/tmp/w32-1-lane/cargo cargo run -q -p xtask -- probe src/w32.rs \
  /tmp/w32-1-lane/frozen.o --crate-type lib --emit obj -C panic=abort -C codegen-units=1 \
  -C opt-level=0
```
-> **exit 0**, `/tmp/w32-1-lane/frozen.o` is **336 bytes** (the same empty-object reading as ①).
```
… probe src/w32.rs /tmp/w32-1-lane/arm.o … --cfg 'feature="windowsnt"'
```
-> **exit 1**, **113 error sites** and no warnings recorded.  **Exactly one of them is in this
unit's own text**: `error[E0425]: cannot find function `ltime`` at the C 607 call site - C 588's
forward declaration whose definition is C 7374, past this slice (Deviations 4).  The other **112 are
mirror-anchored** (by file): `nt/inc/sys/include/socket.rs` **70**, `lib/include/signal.rs` **14**,
`nt/inc/include/dirent.rs` **5**, `nt/inc/include/ms-w32.rs` **4**, `lib/include/sys_time.rs`
**4**, `include/w32.rs` **4**, `lib/include/unistd.in.rs` **4**, `include/dispextern.rs` **4**,
`nt/inc/include/pwd.rs` **2**, `lib/include/acl.rs` **1**.  Three classes: (a) the names the socket
mirror's own note (C 18-22) assigns to *the including unit* (`WSA*` codes, `ws_fd_set`, `u_short`,
`u_long`, `sockaddr`/`hostent`/`servent`/`addrinfo`, `O_NOINHERIT`) - this slice's C text names none
of them, so this unit declares none; (b) **duplicate definitions between two mirrors of one header**
(`lib/include/signal.rs` vs `nt/inc/include/signal.rs`, whose own note records that the MinGW build
reaches MinGW's `<signal.h>` and *not* the gnulib mirror; `lib/include/sys_time.rs` vs
`nt/inc/sys/include/time.rs`; `unistd.in.rs` vs `ms-w32.rs`; `pwd.rs` vs `ms-w32.rs`; `acl.rs` vs
`nt/inc/sys/include/acl.rs`); (c) the four names this unit **defines** that `include/w32.rs` also
declares (`pMultiByteToWideChar`, `pWideCharToMultiByte`, `multiByteToWideCharFlags`,
`w32_get_internal_run_time` - the `E0428` class, owed 6).  None of (a)/(b) is this unit's; all of
(c) is, and it is owed to the mirror.

**③ `extract-abi --check` (P-86 ⑩: no longer vacuous).**
`python3 tools/extract-abi.py --check emacs/src/w32.c femacs/src/w32.rs` -> **FAIL**, **277 `- `
lines**: 204 `missing function`, 34 `missing file-scope object`, 21 `missing macro`, 12
`missing constant`, 5 token lines (`does not mention wchar_t` x3 on C 798/857/912,
`does not mention null` x2 on C 1157/1177) and `line count drifted 83.8%: C 11362 vs Rust 1845`.
Everything but the five token lines is the remainder (C 1198-11362) or the slice's own drift; the
token lines are the `c_ushort`/`is_none ()` spellings Deviations 9/5 record, and their rows belong
to `docs/ports/token-aliases.tsv` (owed 5).  An earlier revision of this ledger read 280 lines with
`struct _REPARSE_DATA_BUFFER: missing fields ['DUMMYUNIONNAME']` among them; that field is named
C's way in the final revision (Deviations 9) and the two lines are gone.

**④ The arm-reach pre-flight, and two mutation tests (P-86 ⑦) - a *different* reading.**
The probe materialization of the final revision, with **12 mirror copies patched in `/tmp`** and
nothing else changed: 58 stand-in items (50 in the socket mirror, 3 in `dirent.rs`, 3 in
`dispextern.rs`, 1 in `ms-w32.rs`, 1 `ltime` in the unit's own copy), 63 duplicate renames (so the
second definition of a name is no longer a redefinition) and 20 removals (14 in the socket mirror's
`fd_set`/`F_*`/`O_*` block, 3 `sigaction` layout assertions in `nt/inc/include/signal.rs`, 1
`#[derive]`, `verror`'s `VaList` line in `include/lisp.rs`):
```
rustc --edition 2024 --crate-type lib --emit obj -C panic=abort -C codegen-units=1 -C opt-level=0 \
  --cfg 'feature="windowsnt"' /tmp/w32-1-lane/harness/_root.rs -o /tmp/w32-1-lane/preflight/arm-final.o
-> exit 0, 0 errors, 15 warnings, 15224-byte object
```
**Two mutation tests** (the unit's own text, in the same harness):
```
C 573  `if g_b_init_is_windows_9x == 0`  -> `== "x"`
  -> exit 1, error[E0308] at _root.rs:1160: `expected `i32`, found `&str``
C 634  `LoadLibrary (c"Advapi32.dll".as_ptr ())` -> `LoadLibrary (1)`
  -> exit 1, error[E0308] at _root.rs:1233: `expected `*const i8`, found `usize``
```
So the pre-flight really type-checks this unit's text (not a vacuous "0 errors"), and the readings
above are `/tmp` harness readings: **the mirrors' Windows arm has never been compiled as it stands,
and no Windows target was used** (P-86 ⑦'s caveat).

**Cross-check of the final revision.**  Three edits landed after the readings above: the C 20-22
attribution comment, and the file gate on the two SDK macros (`ZeroMemory`, `CTL_CODE` - both
compose constants that are gated off in this configuration, so neither is *used* by the arm
reading).  `rustc --edition 2024 --crate-type lib --emit obj -C panic=abort -C codegen-units=1 -C
opt-level=0 femacs/src/w32.rs -o /tmp/w32-1-lane/f2.o` (the unit compiled directly, no
materialization) -> **exit 0, 336 bytes**, and the same command with `--cfg 'feature="windowsnt"'`
-> exit 1 with **no `ZeroMemory`/`CTL_CODE` error line** and the same error-class census (the
*unmaterialized* graph reports 702 mirror errors of its own, a different harness shape and not
comparable with Evidence ②'s 112).  The pre-flight's `exit 0` reading of Evidence ④ is therefore
for the revision before those two `#[cfg]` lines, and the direct reading is what the final revision
has.

## Deviations
- **The `<windows.h>`/SDK section is spelled, not measured.**  C 101, 134-139, 161-163 and 226-263
  name headers with no file in the reference tree (P-88 ①: the declaration site is C's own
  `#include` line, and no file is added under `femacs/src/include/`).  Every scalar is PORTING.md
  1.1's fixed width (`DWORD`/`LONG`/`ULONG`/`UINT` are the 32-bit Windows types whatever this
  target's `c_long` is; `HANDLE` and the SDK's handle records are `void *`), every `#[repr(C)]`
  record is **UNVERIFIED**, and **no `const _` layout assertion is written** for any of them -
  the assertion needs a compiler that has the SDK, and the machine has none (the `w32fns.rs` 10 /
  `w32term.rs` 13 reading of the same situation, where the assertions are owed).
- **`OSVERSIONINFO` and `SYSTEM_INFO` are written here, where `include/w32common.rs` 35-36 and
  `w32fns.rs` 315-319 *defer* them.**  The reason is that this slice embeds both **by value** in
  its own body (C 572's local, C 1174's store) and cannot be transcribed without them; the
  declaration stands at C 101's `#include <windows.h>` line with the SDK's text above it, the
  width is `UNVERIFIED`, and the reconciliation (the mirror's `sysinfo_cache`/`osinfo_cache` then
  resolve, and PORTING.md should have an entry for "an unmeasured SDK record written from its
  citation" - §11) is owed 9.
- **C's `#undef` blocks are carried as C's own text in a comment** (C 51-75 and 227-245).  Their
  target is `nt/inc/ms-w32.h`'s object-like renames, which the mirror spells as `macro_rules!` bound
  at call sites; Rust has no rename to cancel.
- **C's implicit conversions are written out** (PORTING.md 0.3): C 602/C 604's `DWORD`→`LONG`
  (`as LONG`), C 791's `FALSE`→`DWORD`, C 927/C 943/C 964's `ENOTSUP`→`DWORD` (the function returns
  `DWORD`), C 1174's `-1`→`DWORD` (`(-1 as c_int) as DWORD`), C 607's `LONGLONG`→`ULONGLONG`.
- **C's function-pointer cast is a `transmute`**: `(OpenProcessToken_Proc) get_proc_addr (…)` and
  its forty siblings - Rust has no `as` cast to an `Option<fn>` type, and the two pointer widths are
  equal (the `decompress.rs` 237 `LOAD_DLL_FN!` reading).  C's pointer truth tests are the port's
  `.is_some ()`/`.is_none ()` and C's call-through is `.unwrap () (…)`.
- **Three dropped declaration blocks create exactly one unresolved name.**  C 77-89 and C 287-298
  (declarations of functions defined later in the file or in `w32proc.c`) have no Rust item
  (PORTING.md 0.1-12) and this slice calls none of them; C 588's `ltime` **is** called at C 607 and
  its definition is C 7374, so this slice's text alone cannot resolve it - the arm reading's one
  unit-anchored error, stubbed in the pre-flight.
- **C's function-local `static` objects are function-local `static mut`s** (`s_b_ret` C 571, the two
  `zero`s C 722/C 746, every `s_pfn_*`), and C's file-scope `static`s are non-`pub` `static mut`s
  (PORTING.md 0.2); the two `extern`-then-define pairs (C 355-358) keep C's external linkage and the
  `extern` line is recorded.  C's uninitialized locals are zeroed and named (PORTING.md 1.5), and
  the two `MAX_PATH` buffers are `[0; MAX_PATH as usize]`.
- **`wchar_t` is `c_ushort`** (the Windows 16-bit wide character - the `include/w32.rs` 405-418
  reading), which is what the `does not mention wchar_t` lines of Evidence ③ are (owed 5), and the
  `LARGE_INTEGER`/`SYSTEM_INFO`/`REPARSE_DATA_BUFFER` nameless inner structs are named: `u` for the
  two SDK records and `DUMMYUNIONNAME` - C's own spelling, which mingw-w64 defines to nothing - for
  the one C 180-204 declares, so C's `x.MountPointReparseBuffer` reads as
  `x.DUMMYUNIONNAME.MountPointReparseBuffer` there.
- **`DEFER_MS_W32_H` (C 24) is a boolean constant**, the shape the fleet's mirrors use for a
  compile-time switch (`nt/inc/include/ms-w32.rs`'s `WINDOWSNT`/`MINGW_W64`), and **`ORIG_WIN32_WINNT`
  (C 249-260) has no Rust item** - it is preprocessor state, not a value the port has; its C text is
  kept and the row for `docs/ports/excluded-macros.txt` (kind=config) is owed 4.
- **The include graph picks the header `nt/inc` overrides** (`<dirent.h>`, `<grp.h>`, `<pwd.h>`,
  `<signal.h>`, `<sys/acl.h>`, `<sys/file.h>`, `<sys/socket.h>`, `<sys/time.h>`, `<mingw_time.h>`,
  `<ms-w32.h>`): that is what a MinGW build resolves those names to, and those mirrors' own notes
  name `src/w32.c` as one of the units that reaches them.  The five headers with no mirror
  (`<stdarg.h>`, `<io.h>`, `<mbstring.h>`, `<sys/utime.h>`, `<windows.h>`) and the SDK headers of
  C 134-163 are recorded at their C lines.
- **Line count.**  C 1,197 (this slice) vs the port's 1,846 (+54.2%) - a
  `docs/ports/line-drift-tolerance.tsv` row for the stem `w32` is owed (basis `total`), derived from
  the finished prefix the way that table's own note describes for a half-ported unit.

### The second slice's deviations (C 1199-4173)

- **The `<windows.h>`/SDK section grows by the second slice's names, and every one is spelled from
  the SDK's text, not measured.**  `CPINFO`, `ULARGE_INTEGER`, `LUID`/`LUID_AND_ATTRIBUTES`/
  `TOKEN_PRIVILEGES`, `SID_AND_ATTRIBUTES`/`TOKEN_USER`/`TOKEN_PRIMARY_GROUP`, `WIN32_FIND_DATAA`/
  `WIN32_FIND_DATAW`, `NETRESOURCEA`/`NETRESOURCEW`, `_utimbuf`, `HCRYPTPROV`, `LCID` and the ~40
  SDK/CRT entry points and ~60 constants stand in the same section (C 101's `#include <windows.h>`
  and its relatives), each with the SDK's own line above it; **no `const _` layout assertion is
  written** for any of them (the machine has no `windows.h`), and the assertions are owed (below).
  This is the same reading `w32fns.rs` 10 / `w32term.rs` 13 record.
- **`_fsize_t` is declared by the *unit*, at C 270's `#include <dirent.h>` line.**  C's `struct
  dirent` (the `nt/inc/dirent.h` mirror) names it in `d_size` while that mirror deliberately
  declares no system type of its own ("named from the including unit", its own note) - so the unit
  declares `pub type _fsize_t = c_ulong;` with `<io.h>`'s text; **the width is `UNVERIFIED`** (the
  mirror's row for it is owed).
- **`DBL_EPSILON` is declared by the unit**, although `<float.h>`'s home is
  `lib/include/float.in.rs` - that mirror carries the seven constants other units needed and not
  this one.  The value is the header's own text; the row that moves it into the mirror is owed.
- **`xstrcasecmp` is defined by the unit** (C 274's `dispextern.h`): `include/dispextern.rs`
  2594-2604 keeps its copy of C 104-111's `INLINE` in `dispextern_externs`, a module the including
  unit does not glob-import (so that a unit defining the name does not collide), which leaves each
  user to carry the inline - the same choice `src/font.rs` makes for the same name.
- **`c_longdouble` is a unit-level alias** (`pub type c_longdouble = c_double;`) for `getavg`'s two
  `long double` locals - PORTING.md 1.1's spelling, measured by `src/editfns.rs` 197 and
  `lib-src/ntlib.rs` 81.
- **`alloca`'s four sites are `sa_stack_alloc`** (PORTING.md P-05: C 1560, C 2810, C 2902, C 3936),
  reached through `include/lisp.rs` 3978's declaration - **the unit declares nothing of its own**,
  which is what `tools/check-mirror-dupes.py` requires (its one failure during this batch was that
  duplicate, removed).
- **One C local is renamed, and one C expression is split.**  C 1730's `char *cp = NULL` shadows
  C 1713's `static CPINFO cp` inside the same function; Rust forbids a `let` shadowing a static
  (`E0530`), so the port names the local `cp_` (the only name change in the slice, marked at the
  line).  C 2102's `first_idx = last_idx = -1;` is two statements in C's own order (Rust's chained
  assignment is a `()` expression), and C 2657's `if (cstatus == 0 && (len = …) < size)` is written
  as the assignment followed by the test, which is what C's short-circuit does (the assignment is
  evaluated before the comparison).
- **Three `switch`es have a `FALLTHROUGH` into a shared body and keep C's semantics in another
  shape** (PORTING.md 2.2): `sys_readdir`'s (C 3790-3813) `FALLTHROUGH` into an *empty* `default`
  is a no-op, so the port's `match` keeps C's four arms in the order that stays reachable;
  `map_w32_filename`'s (C 3603-3616) `~`/`#` arm falls into `default`, which the port spells by
  repeating that body; `access_attrs`' (C 4143-4157) `ERROR_INVALID_NAME`/`ERROR_BAD_PATHNAME` arm
  falls into the `ENOENT` group, which the port spells as that group's own statement (the same
  `*errno () = ENOENT`).  `logon_network_drive`'s `switch (val)` (C 4090-4109) has a `default` in
  the middle and is a plain `match` with C's `_` last.
- **C's implicit conversions are written out** (PORTING.md 0.3): the `int`→`DWORD`/`EMACS_INT`
  stores (`w32_ansi_code_page = CP_ACP as EMACS_INT`, C 1688; `w32_num_mouse_buttons = … as
  EMACS_INT`, C 3210), `-1`→`DWORD` (`(-1 as c_int) as DWORD`, C 4009/4148/4151/4169), `size_t`→`int`
  (`strlen (…) as c_int`), the `long double`→`double` store in `getavg` (C 2071), `c_tolower`'s
  `int`→`char` (C 3612), the two `DWORD`→`ULONG`/`UINT` argument conversions, and
  `SSET`'s `char`→`u8`.
- **`countof (samples)` is a const** (`SAMPLES_LEN: usize = 16 * 60`): a `static mut` may not be
  read in a static initializer (Rust's `E0133`), and C's own `16*60` is the expression kept.  The
  array and `max_idx` both use it.
- **The wide literals** `L"HOME"`, `L"SHELL"`, `L"\"` and `L"*"` (C 2366-2367, C 3764-3765) are
  `[WCHAR; N]` arrays of the same code units (Rust has no `L"…"` syntax - the `w32fns.rs` 385
  reading), and `SecureZeroMemory`/`MAKELCID`/`IS_DIRECTORY_SEP`/`DRIVE_INDEX`/
  `VOLINFO_STILL_VALID` are `macro_rules!` of C's own expressions (PORTING.md 2.1).
- **`SecureZeroMemory` (C 2474) and the compiler barrier (C 2479)** are the SDK's `RtlZeroMemory`
  family and `asm volatile ("" ::: "memory")`; the port writes the first as a `macro_rules!` that
  stores zero bytes through a volatile pointer (the property the SDK's builtin exists for) and the
  second as `core::sync::atomic::compiler_fence`, with C's text above each.
- **`EMACS_CONFIGURATION` (C 3158) is the `ms-w32.h` macro** `include/ms-w32.rs` 167 carries
  (`#define EMACS_CONFIGURATION get_emacs_configuration ()`), so the port writes
  `EMACS_CONFIGURATION! ()` - C's own text through that mirror.  **`PATH_EXEC` (C 3085) is the
  frozen build's measured `epaths.h` value**, the same constant `src/callproc.rs` 275 keeps for the
  same generated header; a Windows build's own `epaths.h` is what a Windows target supplies.
- **Two include edges are added to the unit** (both are C's own graph): C 19's `#include <config.h>`
  brings `include/conf_post.rs` (its AH_BOTTOM `#include <conf_post.h>`, which the previous slice's
  include block did not carry - the `w32select.rs` 134 reading), and `lib/include/stdcountof.rs`
  (C 95's `lisp.h` 28 `#include <stdcountof.h>`, which `include/lisp.rs` does not carry) supplies
  `countof!`.  The `#undef` blocks of C 51-75 are unaffected.
- **`EMACS_INT_MAX > INT_MAX` (C 2446) is carried as the arm clang compiles, ungated**, with the
  `#else` text in the comment above it: the evaluator cannot see `EMACS_INT_MAX` (a
  `config.h`-dependent macro) and the frozen build's clang answers True, and PORTING.md P-02 fixes
  the wide-integer ABI for every supported target (the `fns.rs` C 5383 reading).
- **Line count.**  C 1-4,173 (this slice's share: 2,975 lines) vs the port's 6,332 (+51.8% over the
  whole prefix): a `docs/ports/line-drift-tolerance.tsv` row for the stem `w32` is owed
  (basis `total`).

### The third slice's deviations (C 4174-4395)

- **C's parenthesised conditions keep both pairs.**  `faccessat`'s C 4229/4235/4240 and
  `sys_access`'s C 4377/4384/4389 are `if ((mode & X_OK) != 0 …)`: the outer pair wraps the whole
  condition, and the port carries it as written (the first attempt dropped it and rustc's
  "unexpected closing delimiter" at the multi-line ones proved the pair belongs to C, not to the
  precedence the port needs).
- **C 4191-4193's `if (_snprintf (…) < 0)`** likewise keeps C's outer pair, and the variadic call
  passes `(&raw mut fullname) as *mut c_char`, `core::mem::size_of_val (&fullname)` for C's
  `sizeof fullname`, and the ternary as an `if` **expression** whose two branches are `c"".as_ptr
  ()` / `c"/".as_ptr ()`.
- **C 4189's `char lastc = dir_pathname[strlen (dir_pathname) - 1]`** is the port's
  `*dir_pathname.offset ((strlen (dir_pathname) - 1) as isize)`; **C 4198's `path = fullname`** is
  the array-decay cast `(&raw mut fullname) as *const c_char`; **C 4204's `mode |= D_OK`** and
  C 4203's `path[strlen (path) - 1]` are the same two devices.
- **C 4191/4359's assignments inside conditions** are two statements in the port (`attributes =
  access_attrs (path);` then `if attributes == -1 as DWORD`, and the same for
  `GetFileAttributesA`), because Rust has no assignment expression; C's `-1` against a `DWORD` is
  the port's `-1 as DWORD`.
- **The `<winnt.h>`/`<errno.h>` names this slice first spells** are declared at their first use
  site (the P-88 device this lane already uses): `FILE_ATTRIBUTE_REPARSE_POINT 0x00000400`,
  `FILE_SUPPORTS_REPARSE_POINTS 0x00040000`, `ERROR_NO_MORE_FILES 18`, `ERROR_NO_MORE_ITEMS 259`
  and `ENODEV 19`, each with the ledger's `UNVERIFIED` marking (no Windows SDK or mingw toolchain
  on this machine - Owed 10's class).
- **`IS_DEVICE_SEP` is a `macro_rules!` carried from C's own generated `<config.h>` expression**
  (the unit's `IS_DIRECTORY_SEP` device): `configure.ac` 6494's Windows branch is
  `((_c_) == ':')` while the frozen `build/emacs-ref/src/config.h` 2094 carries the Darwin branch's
  `0`, and the macro must sit **above** its first use (a `macro_rules!` is textually scoped, which
  the arm reading proved by reporting `cannot find macro IS_DEVICE_SEP` when it was placed below
  `faccessat`).
- **C 4291/4292's `L"\"`/`L"*"`** are the unit's existing `BACKSLASH_W`/`STAR_W` statics
  (`wcscat (pat_w.as_mut_ptr (), BACKSLASH_W.as_ptr ())`), the C 3725-3740 device; the arrays C
  declares are `[c_ushort; MAX_PATH as usize]` / `[c_char; MAX_PATH as usize]` / `… + 2`, and the
  SDK records (`WIN32_FIND_DATAW`/`A`) start zeroed (Rust cannot partially initialise, PORTING.md
  0.1-13).
- **C 4262's `if (is_unc_volume (dirname))`** is the port's `!= 0` test (the function returns
  `c_int`), **C 4275's `read_result != NULL || GetLastError () == ERROR_NO_MORE_ITEMS`** is that
  comparison `as c_int` (C's `int` return), and **C 4221's `!not_a_symlink`** is
  `not_a_symlink == 0` (C's `int` holding a boolean).
- **C 4355-4357's `for (p = fname_copy; *p; p = CharNext (p))`** is the port's `while *p != 0`
  loop with `CharNext (p)` as its step and C's `'/'`/`'\'` as `b'/' as c_char` / `b'\\' as
  c_char`.

### The fourth slice's deviations (C 4396-4533)

- **The nine CRT functions this slice first calls are declared at their first use site** (the P-88
  device): `_chdir`, `_wchdir`, `_chmod`, `_wchmod`, `_creat`, `_wcreat`, `_open` and `_wopen`
  (the last two variadic, as the CRT declares them) and `_fdopen`, each with C's own prototype
  line.  No Windows SDK or mingw toolchain is on this machine, so their signatures carry the
  ledger's `UNVERIFIED` marking (Owed 10's class).
- **`_O_NOINHERIT` is declared in-unit** (`0x0080`, the same `UNVERIFIED` class); **`O_TEXT` and
  `O_BINARY` are *not***: the unit's own C 35 `#include <fcntl.h>` reaches
  `lib/include/fcntl.in.rs`, which carries both (186/187) with the **frozen target's `0`**, while
  the Windows CRT's own values are `0x4000`/`0x8000`.  The first attempt's in-unit copies were two
  `E0428`s; the divergence they would have papered over is recorded as Owed 13 instead.
- **`O_RDONLY`/`O_WRONLY`/`O_RDWR`/`O_CREAT`/`O_TRUNC`** likewise come from that mirror, i.e. with
  the frozen target's numbers (`0/1/2/0x200/0x400`), while the CRT's are `0/1/2/0x100/0x200` - the
  same Owed 13 class, and the reason C 4489/4491's `oflag` values must be read as the *CRT's* on
  the Windows arm.
- **C 4496's `while (*++mode)`** steps and tests in one operator, so the port writes a `loop` whose
  first two statements are the step (`mode = mode.offset (1);`) and the test
  (`if *mode == 0 { break; }`), and the `mode` parameter is therefore a `mut` binding while
  `mode_save` holds C's original pointer (the `image.rs` 11103 device).
- **C 4488-4512's `'r'`/`'w'`/`'a'`/`'+'`/`'b'`/`'t'` comparisons** are
  `b'r' as c_char` and friends, and C 4520/4527's `0644` is `0o644`; `oflag &= ~…` is
  `oflag &= ! …`.
- **C 4454's `chmod_worker (path, mode)`** with `mode_t mode` narrows with `mode as c_int` (C's own
  conversion), and C 4477's `FILE *` return is the mirror's `*mut FILE`
  (`lib/include/stdio-impl.rs` 76, reached through the unit's C 31 `#include <stdio.h>`).
- **C 4408/4416/4429/4436/4463/4470/4517/4524's local arrays** are
  `[c_ushort; MAX_PATH as usize]` / `[c_char; MAX_PATH as usize]`, and the wide arms pass
  `path_w.as_mut_ptr ()` / `newdir_w.as_ptr ()` (C's own decay).

### The fifth slice's deviations (C 4534-4673)

- **The `<winbase.h>`/`<winnt.h>` names this slice first spells are declared at its first use
  site** (the P-88 device, all `UNVERIFIED`): `OPEN_EXISTING 3`,
  `FILE_FLAG_BACKUP_SEMANTICS 0x02000000`, `BACKUP_LINK 0x00000005`, `ERROR_NOT_SAME_DEVICE 17`,
  `ERROR_TOO_MANY_LINKS 1142`, `<errno.h>`'s `EMLINK 31`, the `WIN32_STREAM_ID` record (C's
  `ANYSIZE_ARRAY` array spelled as one element, the `LUID_AND_ATTRIBUTES` device) and the three
  `CreateFileA`/`CreateFileW`/`BackupWrite` prototypes with C's own parameter text.
- **C 4577-4580's anonymous local struct** becomes the named record `w32_sys_link_data`
  (`wid` + `wbuffer`), the `LARGE_INTEGER_u` device above; **C 4594's `data.wid.Size.LowPart`** is
  therefore `data.wid.Size.u.LowPart` (the recorded nameless-inner-struct deviation), and its
  `wlen * sizeof (WCHAR)` is spelled `(wlen as c_uint).wrapping_mul (…)` (C's `int` * 2 in an
  assignment to a `DWORD`).
- **C 4599's `offsetof (WIN32_STREAM_ID, cStreamName)`** is `core::mem::offset_of!` (the
  `android.rs` device), added to `DWORD` with `wrapping_add` (C's unsigned addition).
- **C 4601/4602's `FALSE`/`TRUE`** are the `0`/`1` the `BOOL` type spells, and C 4585's second
  argument `0` is the unit's existing `multiByteToWideCharFlags as c_ulong` (the
  `filename_to_utf16` device), so both `BackupWrite` calls read C's arguments through that
  variable.
- **C 4623-4628's two conditions keep C's own outer paren pair** (`if (attributes != -1 …)` /
  `else if (attributes == -1 …)`), the slice-4 lesson; C 4627/4628's `oldname` argument is
  `(&raw const oldname) as *const c_char` (C's array decay) and its comparison to the `-1` of
  `unc_volume_file_attributes` is `!= -1 as DWORD`.
- **C 4589/4590's locals** are declared where C declares them (`let mut context: LPVOID` /
  `let mut wbytes: DWORD = 0;`), and C 4609/4610's `err`/`attributes` likewise.
- **`_mkdir`/`_wmkdir` are declared in-unit** (the slice-4 CRT device) and **C 4654's `mode_t mode`
  is unused** by C's body, so the port keeps the parameter unused (the file-wide `allow
  (unused_variables)`).

### The sixth slice's deviations (C 4674-4782)

- **C 4676's `...` and its `va_list` use follow P-04** (`docs/spikes/2026-10-02-variadics.md` 37):
  the definition is `pub unsafe extern "C" fn sys_open (path: *const c_char, oflag: c_int, mut ap:
  ...) -> c_int`, C 4680's `va_list ap;` has no declaration, C 4683's `va_start` and C 4726's
  `va_end` have no statement, and C 4684's `va_arg (ap, int)` is `ap.next_arg::<c_int> ()`.
- **C 4731-4754's `#if 0` block is recorded, not carried** (dead in every configuration, the
  `android.c` 1124-1360 device): the frame comment names C 4731-4732's `DO NOT DELETE!` note and
  the `openat` body it guards.
- **C 4694/4712's `(oflag & (_O_CREAT | _O_EXCL))`** uses the **CRT's** names, which no mirror
  carries, so `_O_CREAT 0x0100` and `_O_EXCL 0x0200` are declared in-unit (`UNVERIFIED`, Owed 13's
  class); `INVALID_FILE_ATTRIBUTES` (`(DWORD) -1`) likewise, and C 4702/4720's comparison is
  `!= INVALID_FILE_ATTRIBUTES` while C 4698/4716's is `*errno () == EACCES`.
- **C 4695/4713's `(oflag & ~_O_CREAT) | _O_NOINHERIT`** is `(oflag & ! _O_CREAT) | _O_NOINHERIT`,
  and C 4691-4693/4709's locals and comments keep C's positions.
- **C 4781's `flags == AT_SYMLINK_NOFOLLOW ? lchmod (path, mode) : sys_chmod (path, mode)`** is the
  port's `if` expression, with `mode as c_int` at the `sys_chmod` call (C's `mode_t`-into-`int`);
  C 4771-4772's `_snprintf (…) < 0` keeps C's own outer paren pair (the slice-4/5 lesson) and C
  4777's `path = fullname` the array-decay cast.
- **`fchmod`/`fchmodat`'s bodies are C's own** (C 4759's `return 0;` and the dispatch), and their
  `mode_t` parameters are kept as declared.

### The seventh slice's deviations (C 4783-5013)

- **C 4989's `return_result:` label and its eight `goto return_result` jumps** (C 4901/4913/4918/
  4921/4960/4972/4977/4980) are the port's labelled block: the jumps are `break 'return_result`,
  the block carries the two big arms, and C 4990-5004's label tail follows the block (the
  `androidmenu.rs` `'finish` shape); `result`/`have_temp_a`/`temp_a`/`oldname_a` stay in the
  function's scope so that tail can read them.
- **C 60's `#undef rename` matters**: `nt/inc/ms-w32.h` 223's `#define rename sys_rename` is undone
  at the top of `w32.c`, so C 4846/4941/4982/5002's `rename` calls are the **CRT's** function, not
  Emacs's `sys_rename` (which this slice also defines, C 5009-5013).  The unit's own `<stdio.h>`
  reach (`lib/include/stdio.rs`) does not carry `rename` - `lib/include/stdio.in.rs` 277 does, but
  that mirror is not in this unit's chain - so the port declares `rename` in-unit (P-88,
  `UNVERIFIED`) and calls it at those four sites.  (`_rename`, the first guess, was wrong: the
  checker's `rename` token row and the mirror's own declaration are what corrected it.)
- **C 4788's `char temp_a[MAX_PATH + 15]`** keeps C's `"+ 15": pacify GCC` comment and is
  `[c_char; MAX_PATH as usize + 15]`.
- **C 4830-4838's assignment-in-condition `strrchr` pair** splits into the assignment plus
  `if ! o.is_null ()` / `if ! p.is_null ()`, with C's `o++`/`p++` as `o = o.offset (1);`; C 4833/
  4838's `o = oldname_a` / `p = temp_a` are the array-decay casts.
- **C 4840-4849's `do { … } while (result < 0 && errno == EEXIST);`** is the port's `loop` whose
  tail is `if ! (result < 0 && *errno () == EEXIST) { break; }`, and C 4845's `i++` is `i += 1;`.
- **C 4819/4869's `volume_info.serialnum`** (a `DWORD`) narrows with `as c_int` into C's two `int`
  locals, and C 4813/4925/4984's `is_symlink` calls cast the array to `*const c_char`.
- **The CRT names this slice first calls** - `_wrename`, `_rmdir`, `_wrmdir`, `_unlink`, `_wunlink`
  - and `ERROR_PRIVILEGE_NOT_HELD 1314` are declared in-unit (P-88, `UNVERIFIED`); C 4900/4959's
  `0666` is `0o666`, and C 4891/4902/4903/4950/4961/4962's assignment-in-condition
  `GetFileAttributes*` calls keep C's condition shape with the assignment split out.
- **`is_symlink` has no item yet** (C 807's prototype; its definition is past this slice), so the
  arm reading names its three call sites - the `ltime`/`chase_symlinks` class, recorded here rather
  than stubbed.

### The eighth slice's deviations (C 5014-5148)

- **C 5056/5071's `is_symlink (path) & FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY`** converts C's `int` return to the
  `DWORD` the SDK macro is (`(is_symlink (path) as DWORD) & FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY`), and C
  5051/5068's `e = errno` / `errno = e` pair is `e = *errno ();` / `*errno () = e;`.
- **The three file-scope statics** carry C's own comments (`static mut utc_base_ft: FILETIME`,
  `static mut utc_base: ULONGLONG`, `static mut init: c_int`), each gated with the rest of this
  file's SDK-typed text.
- **C 5084-5090's `FILETIME_TO_U64`** is a `macro_rules!` whose body is a block (PORTING.md 2.1's
  do-while device); C's `uiTemp.LowPart`/`.HighPart` are the port's `uiTemp.u.LowPart`/`.u.HighPart`
  (the nameless-inner-struct deviation the `LARGE_INTEGER` row already records), and the macro is
  used at C 5107/5125 exactly where C uses it.
- **The SDK records and two calls this slice first names are declared in-unit** (P-88,
  `UNVERIFIED`): the `SYSTEMTIME` record (C's eight `WORD` fields), `SystemTimeToFileTime` and
  `CompareFileTime` with C's own prototype text.
- **C 5110/5133's by-value parameters** stay by value (`ft: FILETIME`, `time: timespec`), and C
  5127-5128's arithmetic is `(tmp / 10000000) as time_t` / `((tmp - time_sec as ULONGLONG *
  10000000) * 100) as c_int` (C's own casts into the `int *time_nsec` store).
- **C 5049/5066's `0666`** is `0o666` (the slice-4/7 device), and the wide arms pass
  `path_w.as_mut_ptr ()` (C's decay).

### The ninth slice's deviations (C 5149-5202)

- **The four `<winnt.h>`/`<accctrl.h>` names this slice first uses are declared at its first use
  site** (P-88, `UNVERIFIED`): `OWNER_SECURITY_INFORMATION 0x00000001`,
  `GROUP_SECURITY_INFORMATION 0x00000002`, `SE_FILE_OBJECT` (the `SE_OBJECT_TYPE` member, `1`) and
  `ERROR_SUCCESS 0`.
- **`get_security_info`, `get_file_security`, `get_sid_sub_authority_count` and
  `get_sid_sub_authority` are this unit's own C 700-830 wrappers** (carried in slice 1), so this
  slice calls the port's own signatures: `get_security_info`'s eight arguments, the
  `get_file_security (…) == 0` test for C's `!get_file_security (…)`, and
  `get_sid_sub_authority (sid, n_subauthorities - 1)` (C's `unsigned`-into-`DWORD`).
- **C 5198's `*get_sid_sub_authority_count (sid)`** is the `PUCHAR` deref narrowed with `as c_uint`
  (C's `unsigned char`-into-`unsigned`), and C 5153/5169's `PSECURITY_DESCRIPTOR psd = NULL` is
  `std::ptr::null_mut ()`.
- **C 5181's `psd = xmalloc (sd_len)`** is `xmalloc (sd_len as usize) as PSECURITY_DESCRIPTOR`
  (C's `void *`-into-the-handle-type), and C 5184's `xfree (psd)` keeps the mirror's signature.
- **C 5155-5156/5171-5172's `SECURITY_INFORMATION si = OWNER… | GROUP…;`** keeps C's
  `/* | DACL_SECURITY_INFORMATION */` comment and the two-flag OR, declared in C's position.

### The tenth slice's deviations (C 5203-5331)

- **C 5206-5211's `struct w32_id`** is a `#[repr(C)]` record whose `sid` member is
  `[c_uchar; 0]` (C's `FLEXIBLE_ARRAY_MEMBER`, the `comp.rs` 884 device), and the `name` member is
  `[c_char; GNLEN + 1]` - `GNLEN`/`UNLEN` are the mirror's `usize` constants
  (`w32.rs` 1752/1754), so the arithmetic stays in `usize` and C 5290's `name_len > UNLEN+1` is
  `name_len > (UNLEN + 1) as DWORD`.
- **C 5220's `for (found = NULL, tail = w32_idlist; tail; tail = tail->next)`** with its `break` is
  the port's two initialiser statements plus `while ! tail.is_null ()`, the body's `break` staying
  a `break`; **C 5222's `(PSID)tail->sid`** is `(&raw mut (*tail).sid) as PSID` (the flexible
  array's own address).
- **C 5249's `offsetof (struct w32_id, sid)`** is `core::mem::offset_of! (w32_id, sid)`, added to
  `sid_len as usize` inside `xmalloc`, whose result is cast to `*mut w32_id` (C's `void *`).
- **`dflt_passwd`/`dflt_group` are *values* in the port** (the C 2148-2170 statics the file already
  carries), so C 5323/5324/5328/5329's `dflt_passwd.pw_uid` and `dflt_group.gr_name` are read
  exactly as C writes them (no pointer deref), and C 5324/5329's `strcpy` destinations are the
  `st_uname`/`st_gname` arrays cast to `*mut c_char` (C's decay).
- **C 5314/5316's `&st->st_uid`/`&st->st_gid`** are `(&raw mut (*st).st_uid)`/`…st_gid`
  (`c_uint` fields in the Windows `stat` mirror), which is why the `id` parameter is `*mut c_uint`.
- **C 5268-5275's locals** keep C's initialisers (`name_len`/`domain_len` from
  `core::mem::size_of_val`), `SID_NAME_USE ignore` starts zeroed (Rust cannot partially
  initialise, PORTING.md 0.1-13), and C 5288-5290's `!lookup_account_sid (…) || name_len > …` is
  the port's `… == 0 || …` with C's own parens.

### The eleventh slice's deviations (C 5332-5365)

- **C 5334's `int is_slow_fs (const char *);`** is C's own forward declaration of a function this
  slice then defines; the port needs no item for it (Rust resolves forward references), so the
  line is carried in this slice's frame comment (the C 800-812 device).
- **The two `<winbase.h>` drive types are declared at their first use** (P-88, `UNVERIFIED`):
  `DRIVE_FIXED 3` and `DRIVE_RAMDISK 6` (`DRIVE_REMOTE` was in slice 1's block).
- **C 5342-5344's `IS_DIRECTORY_SEP (name[0])` / `IS_DEVICE_SEP (name[1])`** are the port's macro
  calls with C's element access spelled `*name` / `*name.offset (1)` and `as c_int` (the
  `IS_DIRECTORY_SEP` convention the unit's own C 2579-era sites use); the macros return a Rust
  `bool`, so C's `!(a && b)` needs no `!= 0`.
- **C 5350-5351's `drive_root[2] = '\';` / `[3] = '\0';`** are `b'\' as c_char` and `0`, and C
  5349's `strncpy (drive_root, name, 2)` is the array cast plus the mirror's `strncpy`.
- **C 5354's `return !(devtype == DRIVE_FIXED || devtype == DRIVE_RAMDISK);`** is the port's
  `(! (…) ) as c_int` (the parentheses matter: `as` binds tighter than `!` in Rust).
- **C 5360's `int w32_stat_get_owner_group;`** is a *non-`static`* tentative definition, so the
  port writes `#[unsafe(no_mangle)] pub static mut w32_stat_get_owner_group: c_int = 0;` (the
  C 592/597 file-scope-object convention this unit already uses).

### The twelfth slice's deviations (C 5362-5780)

- **C 5431-5434's condition is C's own text with C's parens.**  The port writes `if (! (NILP
  (Vw32_get_true_file_attributes) || (EQ (Vw32_get_true_file_attributes, Qlocal ()) && is_slow_fs
  (name) != 0)) || (is_a_symlink != 0 && follow_symlinks != 0))`: `NILP`/`EQ` are the unit's own
  macros (they return Rust `bool`, so no `!= 0`), `Qlocal ()` and `Vw32_get_true_file_attributes`
  come from `include/globals.rs` (the `pub mod variables` re-export at its 7208), and C's
  `is_a_symlink`/`follow_symlinks` are the port's `!= 0` tests.  The outer `(` … `)` pair is C's and
  it matters: `as` binds tighter than `!` in Rust, and dropping it makes the negation apply to the
  wrong side (`is_slow_fs` line 11's lesson).
- **C 5466-5467's `goto no_true_file_attributes` and C 5575's fall-through are one device.**  C's
  jump leaves Plan A's block *into* the `else` arm's first statement (C 5578's label), and C's
  fall-through at 5575 leaves the whole if/else for C 5735's tail.  Rust has neither, so the port
  wraps **both arms** in the labelled block `'plan_b`, writes C 5466's test **inverted** (`if fh !=
  INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE`, with the device's brace carrying the comment that C's `goto` has no
  counterpart) and puts `break 'plan_b;` where C 5575's `}` is: the inverted test's `else`-side
  falls out of the `if` arm into Plan B (C's `goto`), and the `break` skips Plan B for the tail
  (C's fall-through).  Plan B's statements are therefore one level left of C's text - the price of
  the device, and the reason the three C 5725-5730 lines carry their own markers.  The
  `androidselect.rs` 414 `'fail` / `androidmenu.rs` 465 `'next_item` blocks are the precedent
  (PORTING.md 2.2).
- **C 5510 and C 5529's `switch`es are `match`es.**  C 5510's one-case `switch (w32err)` keeps
  `case ERROR_FILE_NOT_FOUND: /* can this ever happen? */` and gains the `_ => {}` arm Rust needs;
  C 5529's `switch (ftype)` keeps C's four labels and their order, with C 5537-5539's three labels
  sharing one body as `FILE_TYPE_CHAR | FILE_TYPE_UNKNOWN | _ =>`, and C's `break;` statements have
  no counterpart (the `ntlib.rs` 1005 device).  Both are in the `macro-body-notes.tsv` row below.
- **C 5405's bare `alloca` is `sa_stack_alloc`** (P-05, landed 2026-10-04): `name = strcpy
  (sa_stack_alloc ((len + 2) as usize) as *mut c_char, name);` (`haikuterm.rs` 4947 / `editfns.rs`
  2477 are the precedent).
- **C 5396's `"*?|<>""`** is the c-string `c"*?|<>""` (C's escape is a double quote, not the
  apostrophe the first generator draft emitted), and C 5393's `save_name = name = (char *)
  map_w32_filename (path, &path)` is two port lines (`save_name = map_w32_filename (path, (&raw mut
  path));` then `name = save_name;`) - the `(&raw mut path)` is what makes the parameter `mut`.
- **The three `S_I*` names are declared in-unit (P-88, `UNVERIFIED`)**: `<sys/stat.h>`'s
  `S_IREAD 0x0100`, `S_IWRITE 0x0080`, `S_IEXEC 0x0040` (`nt/inc/sys/include/stat.rs` 44-46 mirrors
  the same three numbers).  The `S_IF*` names C 5522-5540/5726-5730 use are **already in scope** -
  from the POSIX `lib/include/sys_stat.in.rs` (reached through `lib/include/acl.rs`), whose
  `S_IFLNK` is `0o120000` where the MSVC `nt/inc` mirror has `0x0800`; the unit writes C's name and
  the include-graph question stays owed 9's business, not this slice's.
- **Every `st_mode`/`st_dev`/`st_nlink`/`st_size` store carries the mirror's cast** -
  `S_IFLNK as c_ushort` (`stat`'s `st_mode` is `c_ushort`), `serialnum as dev_t`, `nlinks as
  c_short`, `fs_high`/`fs_low as c_ulonglong`, and C 5776's `buf->st_mode |= permission |
  (permission >> 3) | (permission >> 6);` is `(*buf).st_mode |= (permission | (permission >> 3) |
  (permission >> 6)) as c_ushort;`.  `lib-src/ntlib.rs` 1040-1081 writes the same six lines the same
  way (the same `stat` mirror, the same C tail in `lib-src/ntlib.c` 466-505), which is why this
  slice follows it name for name.
- **C 5612's `fattrs == -1`** keeps the unit's landed device (`-1 as DWORD`, Deviations of slice 4;
  the flag is C's promotion of `-1` against the `DWORD`).  **C 5615/5629's `ctime = atime = wtime =
  utc_base_ft;`** is three assignments (Rust has no chained assignment in an expression), **C
  5620-5621's `strcpy (name + len, "\")`** is the `.offset (len as isize)` form plus the c-string,
  and **C 5370-5376/5383's locals** are declared then zeroed (PORTING.md 1.5; the arm has no
  uninitialised locals), exactly as the earlier slices do.
- **`stat_worker` is C's `static int`, so the port writes plain `unsafe fn`** (no
  `#[unsafe(no_mangle)]`, no `pub`) - the `is_slow_fs` contrast one item above it, and the reason
  the `--check` counter moves on the *function* row only.

### The thirteenth slice's deviations (C 5781-6107)

- **C's six definitions are exported** (`stat`, `lstat`, `fstatat`, `fstat`, `utimensat`,
  `sys_umask` are non-`static` in C), so the port writes `#[unsafe(no_mangle)] pub unsafe extern
  "C" fn` for each - the `stat_worker` contrast (a `static int` goes plain `unsafe fn`).
- **C 5833-5855's `switch` with `goto non_disk` and the `non_disk:` label** is the port's `match`
  plus a `non_disk` flag and an `if non_disk != 0` tail: C's `break` (C 5842) is the end of the
  `FILE_TYPE_DISK` arm, C 5845's `goto` and C 5849's fall-through both set the flag, and the
  label's five statements are the tail.  This is the **same device `femacs/lib-src/ntlib.rs`
  1005/1029 uses for the same C body** (`lib-src/ntlib.c` 415-505), which is why this slice
  follows that port name for name - its `st_nlink as c_short`, `st_dev as dev_t`,
  `st_size as c_ulonglong`, `S_IF* as c_ushort` and `st_mode |= (…) as c_ushort` casts are the
  mirror's widths, and the arm's `~` is Rust's `!`.
- **Rust needs braces where C does not**: C 5804, 5983, 5995, 5997, 6001, 6007, 6022, 6032, 6039
  and 6097 are single un-braced statements under an `if`/`else`, so each is written `{ … }` (the
  body keeps C's text; only the braces are added).  **C 6103's `~S_IWRITE`** is Rust's
  `! S_IWRITE` (Rust has no `~`).
- **C's `switch (err)` at C 6057 is a `match`**: the six-error `ENOENT` group keeps C's order and
  shares one arm (`ERROR_FILE_NOT_FOUND | ERROR_PATH_NOT_FOUND | ERROR_INVALID_DRIVE |
  ERROR_BAD_NETPATH | ERROR_DEV_NOT_EXIST | ERROR_INVALID_NAME`), then `ENFILE`, then the two
  `EACCES` errors, then C's `default:` as `_`; C's four `break;` statements have no counterpart.
  The `switch (GetFileType …)` of `fstat` and `switch (w32err)` of slice 12 are the family.  This
  is the slice's `macro-body-notes.tsv` row.
- **C 5978's `ltimes[0] = ltimes[1] = current_timespec ();` calls the function once**, so the port
  writes `let ts = current_timespec (); ltimes[0] = ts; ltimes[1] = ts;` - two calls would be a
  different program (the two timestamps would differ).  The same reading is why C 5615/5629's
  chained `ctime = atime = wtime = utc_base_ft;` is three statements (a value, no call).
- **`utimensat`'s parameter is `*const timespec`** for C's `const struct timespec times[2]`: the
  literal `*const [timespec; 2]` spelling puts a `;` **before** the body's brace, and
  `extract-abi.py`'s body scanner reads a `;` before the first `{` as "this is a declaration"
  (measured: `utimensat` stayed on the `missing function` list until the type was changed).  The
  mirror declares the same four parameters the same way (`lib/include/sys_stat.in.rs` 204).
- **C 5927-5935's `#if 0` region is dead text carried as C's own text** inside the `else` block
  (the C 3390-3406 device), so C's `else` body has no statement in any configuration.
- **The names this slice first uses are declared in-unit** (P-88, `UNVERIFIED`): `<io.h>`'s
  `_get_osfhandle` (`isize`) and `_umask`, `<winbase.h>`'s `SetFileTime` with
  `FILE_TYPE_REMOTE 0x8000`, `FILE_WRITE_ATTRIBUTES 0x00000100`, `FILE_SHARE_READ/WRITE/DELETE`,
  `<winerror.h>`'s `ERROR_DEV_NOT_EXIST 55`/`ERROR_TOO_MANY_OPEN_FILES 4`/
  `ERROR_SHARING_VIOLATION 32`, and two names **no mirror carries**: `AT_EMPTY_PATH` (8192, the
  value `nt/inc/include/unistd.rs` 34 mirrors - `AT_FDCWD`/`AT_SYMLINK_NOFOLLOW` come from the
  `<fcntl.h>` mirror, which is Deviations 13 of slice 4's divergence family) and `ENFILE` (23;
  the `<errno.h>` mirror carries `EMFILE` but not `ENFILE`).  `current_timespec`/`timespec` come
  from C 273's `"systime.h"` (`femacs/src/include/systime.rs` -> `lib/include/timespec.rs`), and
  `S_IREAD`/`S_IWRITE`/`S_IEXEC` are slice 12's in-unit declarations.
- **Other C-isms**: `memset (ltimes, 0, sizeof (ltimes))`, `sizeof (info)` and
  `sizeof (buf->st_ino)` are `core::mem::size_of_val (&x)`; `wchar_t name_utf16[MAX_PATH]` is
  `[c_ushort; MAX_PATH]` (the `token-aliases.tsv` `wchar_t` row); `_snprintf (fullname, …)` is
  the landed `faccessat` spelling (C 4189-4198, same C block) with C's own outer paren pair on
  the `< 0` test; `!SetFileTime (…)` and `!GetFileInformationByHandle (…)` are `… == 0` (the
  unit's negation convention); C 6088's function-local `static int current_mask` is a
  function-local `static mut` (the C 569-586 device); C's `DWORD err = GetLastError ();` and
  `struct timespec` locals are declarations the port zeroes where C leaves them uninitialised
  (PORTING.md 1.5).

### The fourteenth slice's deviations (C 6108-6473)

- **Two of this slice's three definitions are exported** (`symlink` C 6118 and `readlink` C 6331
  are non-`static`), so both are `#[unsafe(no_mangle)] pub unsafe extern "C" fn`; `is_symlink`
  (C 6252) is C's `static int` and stays a plain `unsafe fn` - which is what retires the six
  unresolved `is_symlink` rows the arm reading had carried since slice 1.
- **C 6125-6129's ternary is Rust's `if … { … } else { 0 }`** with C's own outer paren pair around
  the `os_subtype`/version test kept: dropping it is a *syntax* error (the frozen probe caught it
  on this slice's first run), because the closing parens of `(w32_major_version > 10 || (… ))`
  then have no opener - the slice 4/7/8/13 class.
- **C 6111-6116's two `#ifndef` guards are `pub const`s** carrying C's values (the
  `SDDL_REVISION_1` device of C 168-172: "we define them by hand if not already defined"), and
  they are the two names this file's `missing macro`/`missing constant` counters carried.
- **`IS_ANY_SEP` is a generated `<config.h>` macro, not one this file defines**: the port declares
  it in-unit next to `IS_DIRECTORY_SEP!`/`IS_DEVICE_SEP!` (the `IS_DEVICE_SEP!` device, slice 3's
  Deviations) and cites both of `configure.ac`'s branches - 6496's
  `(IS_DIRECTORY_SEP (_c_) || IS_DEVICE_SEP (_c_))` (the Windows build, which is this unit's) and
  6506's `(IS_DIRECTORY_SEP (_c_))` (the frozen target's).
- **`SE_BACKUP_NAME`** is `&core::ffi::CStr` and call sites pass `.as_ptr ()` (the
  `SE_CREATE_SYMBOLIC_LINK_NAME` section's device, and its `unicode` arm is owed the same way).
- **`create_symbolic_link` is the port's own `unsafe extern "system" fn` returning `BOOLEAN`**, so
  C's `!create_symbolic_link (…)` is `… == 0` (the `create_symbolic_link` note above records that
  C's `!retval` reads the same way).
- **C's three `switch`es are `match`es**: C 6202-6232 and C 6277-6289 and C 6451-6464 keep C's arms
  and their order (including `default:` sharing a body with the cases above it, which Rust spells
  in the same or-pattern), and C's `break;` statements have no counterpart.  `case
  ERROR_BAD_NETPATH: break;` (C 6279-6280) is an **empty arm** carrying C's own comment.  These
  three functions' rows are in `docs/ports/macro-body-notes.tsv`.
- **C 6432's bare `alloca` is `sa_stack_alloc`** (P-05): `lwname = sa_stack_alloc (((lwname_len + 1)
  as usize) * core::mem::size_of::<WCHAR> ()) as *mut WCHAR;`.  `WCHAR`, `BYTE` and `USHORT` are
  this unit's own C 224-236 aliases (`c_ushort`/`u8`/`c_ushort`), so C's `WCHAR *`/`WCHAR`
  spellings carry over unchanged; C's `wchar_t path_w[MAX_PATH]` is `[c_ushort; MAX_PATH]` (the
  `wchar_t` row of `token-aliases.tsv`).
- **Pointer arithmetic and casts**: C's `p - linkfn` pointer differences are
  `p.offset_from (linkfn.as_mut_ptr ())` (the unit's landed idiom), `p[-1]` is `*p.offset (-1)`,
  `filename[0]`/`filename[1]` are `*filename`/`*filename.offset (1)`, C 6174's `tem + (p - linkfn)`
  is `tem.as_mut_ptr ().offset (p.offset_from (…))`, and C's `attrs == -1` (a `DWORD`) keeps the
  `-1 as DWORD` device (C 4211's, slice 4).
- **`DeviceIoControl`'s last parameter is `*mut c_void`** where C's prototype says
  `LPOVERLAPPED`: C 6410 passes `NULL` and this unit never names `OVERLAPPED` (P-72 rc 3: a
  pointer to a record the unit does not need), so the port declares the parameter that way and the
  ledger records it.  `MAXIMUM_REPARSE_DATA_BUFFER_SIZE`, `IO_REPARSE_TAG_SYMLINK` and the five
  `<winerror.h>` codes of C 6206-6232/6453-6463 are in-unit declarations (P-88, `UNVERIFIED`).
- **The locals C leaves uninitialised are declared then zeroed** (PORTING.md 1.5):
  `tgtfn`, `dir_access`, `filename_ends_in_slash` (`symlink`), `attrs`, `wfdw`, `wfda`, `fh`,
  `attrs_mean_symlink` (`is_symlink`), `path`, `privs`, `sh`, `retval`, `retbytes`, `lwname`,
  `lname_size` (`readlink`).  C's `filename_w`/`filename_a`/`path_w`/`path_a`/`wfdw`/`wfda`
  lowercase names are kept as C spells them.

### The fifteenth slice's deviations (C 6474-6679)

- **Six of this slice's seven definitions are exported** (`readlinkat` C 6475,
  `symlinks_supported` C 6603, `acl_valid` C 6616, `acl_to_text` C 6622, `acl_from_text` C 6652,
  `acl_free` C 6674 - none is `static`), so each is `#[unsafe(no_mangle)] pub unsafe extern "C"
  fn`; `chase_symlinks` is C's `static char *` and stays a plain `unsafe fn` returning
  `*mut c_char` - which is what retires the arm reading's last two `chase_symlinks` rows.
- **C 6520's function-local `static char target[MAX_UTF8_PATH];`** is a function-local
  `static mut` array (the `map_w32_filename` `shortname` device, slice 2's), and its uses are the
  `as_mut_ptr ()`/`as_ptr ()` forms that device spells.
- **C 6551-6592's `do { … } while (res > 0 && link_len > 0 && ++loop_count <= 100);`** is
  PORTING.md P-95's `loop { … if ! (COND) { break; } }`, and the `++loop_count` - which C evaluates
  only when `&&`'s left side holds - sits in a **block inside the condition**
  (`res > 0 && link_len > 0 && { loop_count += 1; loop_count <= 100 }`), so the port keeps the
  short-circuit exactly; the `100`-crossing test at C 6594-6595 is the second reader of
  `loop_count` and is unchanged.
- **C 6556's `link[link_len--] = '\0';`** is two statements - the store at the old index, then the
  decrement - because Rust has no post-decrement in an index expression; C 6567's `link + link_len`
  is `.offset (link_len as isize)`; `p[-1]` is `*p.offset (-1)`; `target[0]`/`target[1]` are
  `*target.as_ptr ()`/`*target.as_ptr ().offset (1)`.  C's three `(char *)` casts on `file` are
  `file as *mut c_char`, and C 6599's `return target;` (an array decaying to `char *`) is
  `target.as_mut_ptr ()`.
- **C 6535/6544's `GetFullPathNameW`/`GetFullPathNameA`** are in-unit declarations (P-88,
  `UNVERIFIED`; C passes `NULL` for the `lpFilePart`, so that parameter is `*mut LPWSTR`/`*mut
  LPSTR` and the call sites pass `std::ptr::null_mut ()`), and their `DWORD` results are stored
  into C's `ssize_t link_len` with `as ssize_t` - the same store C 6548's `strlen` makes.
- **`symlinks_supported` returns C's `bool` as Rust's `bool`** (the `word_boundary_p` precedent in
  `category.rs`), and C 6607's `get_volume_info (file, NULL)` - a port-internal helper returning
  `c_int` - is `… != 0`.
- **`acl_to_text`'s `char * ATTRIBUTE_MALLOC`** return type is `*mut c_char`, with the attribute
  recorded in a doc comment rather than in the type (PORTING.md 8; the `image.rs` 5253 reading of
  the same attribute).
- **The four ACL entries call this unit's own wrappers**, whose signatures are fixed (they are
  `unsafe extern "system" fn` definitions above): `is_valid_security_descriptor (acl) != 0` for
  C's truth test, `convert_sd_to_sddl (acl, SDDL_REVISION_1, flags, (&raw mut str_acl), (&raw mut
  local_size)) != 0` and `convert_sddl_to_sd (acl_str, SDDL_REVISION_1, (&raw mut psd), (&raw mut
  sd_size)) != 0` for C 6636/6661, `LocalFree (str_acl as *mut c_void)` for C 6644's `char *` into
  `HLOCAL`, and `xmalloc (sd_size as usize)` for C 6664's `void *` into `PSECURITY_DESCRIPTOR`
  (with `memcpy (retval, psd, sd_size as usize)` and `*size = local_size` as C writes them).
- **C 174-223's `MINGW_W64` block owns more than the record**: C 206-222's five `#ifndef` guards
  (`FILE_DEVICE_FILE_SYSTEM`, `METHOD_BUFFERED`, `FILE_ANY_ACCESS`, `CTL_CODE` and
  `FSCTL_GET_REPARSE_POINT`) are *inside* it (the `#endif` at C 223 closes C 174), which is why the
  port gates all of them on `mingw_w64` and why `readlink`'s `FSCTL_GET_REPARSE_POINT` is one of
  the arm reading's three SDK rows (Owed 18).  **`DACL_SECURITY_INFORMATION`** is the SDK scalar
  this slice first uses - C gets it from `<winnt.h>` - so it is declared in-unit (P-88,
  `UNVERIFIED`) next to slice 9's `OWNER_SECURITY_INFORMATION`/`GROUP_SECURITY_INFORMATION` pair.
- **C's `int e = errno;`** is a read of the location, so the port writes `let mut e: c_int =
  *errno ();` (the lvalue `*errno ()` stays for C's own assignments); C 6642's `if (size)` is
  `if ! size.is_null ()`.  The locals C leaves uninitialised are declared then zeroed
  (PORTING.md 1.5): `res`/`link_len` (`chase_symlinks`), `str_acl`/`local_size` (`acl_to_text`),
  `psd`/`sd_size` (`acl_from_text`).

### The sixteenth slice's deviations (C 6680-6903)

- **C 6682/6754's `type` parameter is a Rust keyword**, so both signatures spell it `r#type` and
  every use in the two bodies follows (`r#type == ACL_TYPE_ACCESS`, `r#type != ACL_TYPE_DEFAULT`,
  `(r#type != ACL_TYPE_DEFAULT && r#type != ACL_TYPE_ACCESS)`) - the `atimer.rs` 109 device, and
  the one place this slice *had* to change a name.
- **C assigns to the `fname` parameter** (C 6698, C 6782: `fname = chase_symlinks (filename);`),
  so both entry points take it `mut fname` - the `stat_worker (mut path, …)` device (the goal's
  "same names" rule keeps C's parameter name; only the binding gains `mut`).
- **C's `else if (… || … || …)` conditions keep C's outer paren pair** at 6727-6732, 6734-6739 and
  6861-6866: those three pairs are what the frozen probe caught on the first runs (the
  slice 4/7/8/13/14 class).  C 6824-6826's `if (errno != ENOTSUP) err = set_named_security_info
  (…);` is the slice's one un-braced single statement, written `{ … }`.
- **The three names this slice first uses are declared in-unit** (P-88, `UNVERIFIED`):
  `<winbase.h>`'s `SE_TAKE_OWNERSHIP_NAME`/`SE_RESTORE_NAME` as `&core::ffi::CStr` (the
  `SE_BACKUP_NAME`/`SE_CREATE_SYMBOLIC_LINK_NAME` device, with the same owed `unicode` arm) and
  `<winerror.h>`'s `ERROR_INVALID_OWNER 1305`.  Their call sites pass `.as_ptr ()`.
- **The ACL entries call this unit's own SEH wrappers**, whose signatures are fixed above:
  `set_file_security (fname, flags, acl) == 0` for C 6820's `!set_file_security (…)`,
  `set_named_security_info (fname, SE_FILE_OBJECT, flags, psidOwner, psidGroup, pacl,
  std::ptr::null_mut ())` for C 6825's seven arguments (the last is `pSacl: PACL`, C passes
  `NULL`), `get_security_descriptor_owner/group/dacl (…) != 0` for C 6786/6789/6792's truth tests,
  and `get_file_security (fname, si, psd, 0, (&raw mut sd_len)) == 0` for C 6703/6710's `!` tests.
  `psidOwner != std::ptr::null_mut ()` (and its `psidGroup` twin) is C's `&& psidOwner`, and
  `dacl_present != 0` is C's `&& dacl_present`.
- **`xmalloc (sd_len as usize)`** is C 6709's `void *` into `PSECURITY_DESCRIPTOR`;
  `acl_free (acl_from as *mut c_void)`/`acl_free (acl_to as *mut c_void)` are C 6855/6857's
  `char *` into the `void *` parameter; `xstrcasecmp (acl_from, acl_to) == 0` keeps C's
  comparison.
- **`*errno ()` is read where C reads `errno` and written where C assigns it**: `let e: c_int =
  *errno ();` (C 6694), `e = *errno ();` (C 6814), `*errno () = e;` (C 6745/6852/6874) and
  `if flags == 0` for C 6795's `!flags`, `if st != 0` for C 6877's `if (st)`, `if ! current_acl
  .is_null ()` for C 6844's `if (current_acl)`.  C 6832-6833's empty statement (`if (errno ==
  ENOTSUP) ;`) is the port's `{ }`.
- **C 6810/6812's `st++`** are `st += 1;` (Rust has no post-increment), and **C 6841's local
  declaration inside the `else if` block** (`acl_t current_acl = acl_get_file (…);`) is a `let`
  with the same name and the same initialiser.
- **The locals C leaves uninitialised are declared then zeroed** (PORTING.md 1.5): `filename`
  (`acl_get_file`), and `old1`/`old2`/`err`/`psidOwner`/`psidGroup`/`pacl`/`dflt`/`dacl_present`/
  `e`/`filename` (`acl_set_file`).  `acl_errno_valid`'s three `case` labels share one body and
  become one or-pattern arm (`EBUSY | EINVAL | ENOTSUP =>`), with C's `default:` as `_` - the
  slice's `macro-body-notes.tsv` row.

### The seventeenth slice's deviations (C 6904-7186)

- **`careadlinkat` and `w32_copy_file` are non-`static` definitions** (`#[unsafe(no_mangle)] pub
  unsafe extern "C" fn`), the six process helpers are C's `static … WINAPI` and stay
  `unsafe extern "system" fn` (the slice-1 `open_process_token` device).  `careadlinkat`'s
  parameter list is `lib/include/careadlinkat.rs`'s **to the letter** - `fd: c_int, filename:
  *const c_char, buffer: *mut c_char, buffer_size: usize, alloc: *const allocator, preadlinkat:
  Option<unsafe extern "C" fn (c_int, *const c_char, *mut c_char, usize) -> ssize_t>` - and its
  definition shadows that mirror's glob-imported declaration instead of colliding with it
  (PORTING.md 0.1-10, the `timespec_decls` reading), which is why this entry point needs no mirror
  gate while slice 13-16's five did.
- **C 6927's allocator ternary selects between two *function pointers***
  (`(alloc ? alloc->allocate : xmalloc) (link_size)`): the port names the selection in a local of
  the mirror's field type - `let allocate: Option<unsafe extern "C" fn (usize) -> *mut c_void> =
  if alloc.is_null () { Some (xmalloc) } else { (*alloc).allocate };` - and then calls it the way
  C does, blindly (`(allocate.unwrap ()) (link_size as usize)`).  C 6919's `preadlinkat (…)` is the
  same call-through for a parameter.
- **C 6925's `linkname[link_size++] = '\0';`** is two statements - the store at the old index, then
  `link_size += 1;` - because Rust has no post-increment in an index expression (the slice-15
  `link[link_len--]` device).
- **C's `w32_copy_file` keeps its unused-parameter silencer verbatim**: `preserve_ownership =
  preserve_ownership;` (C 6946), so the binding is `mut preserve_ownership` - the `mut` device
  slice 16 used for C's parameter assignments.  `keep_time` is `mut` for the same reason (C 6997
  reads it, C 6946 only mentions `preserve_ownership`).
- **C's `tnow[0] = tnow[1] = current_timespec ();`** (C 7002) calls the function **once** and the
  port keeps that with one temporary (the slice-13 `ltimes` device); `attributes & ~
  FILE_ATTRIBUTE_READONLY` (C 7007/7022) is Rust's `attributes & ! FILE_ATTRIBUTE_READONLY`;
  `acl_errno_valid (*errno ())` is C's `acl_errno_valid (errno)` (the value, not the location), and
  C's `!copy_result`/`!keep_time`/`if (copy_acls)` are `copy_result == 0`/`keep_time == 0`/
  `copy_acls != 0`.
- **The six names this slice first uses are declared in-unit** (P-88, `UNVERIFIED`):
  `<winbase.h>`'s `CopyFileW`/`CopyFileA`, `SetFileAttributesW`/`SetFileAttributesA` and
  `SetLastError`, plus `<winerror.h>`'s `ERROR_ENCRYPTION_FAILED 6000`.
- **The six proxies are the `LOAD_DLL_FN!` shape**: a function-local
  `static mut s_pfn_X: X_Proc = None` (C's `static X_Proc s_pfn_X = NULL`), the `g_b_init_X` flag
  pair, `LoadLibrary (c"Advapi32.dll".as_ptr ())` or `GetModuleHandle (c"kernel32.dll".as_ptr ())`,
  `std::mem::transmute::<VOIDFNPTR, X_Proc> (get_proc_addr (…))`, `.is_none ()` for C's `== NULL`
  and `.unwrap ()` for the call-through - every spelling the landed `open_process_token` (slice 1)
  and its four siblings already use.  C's `SetLastError (ERROR_NOT_SUPPORTED)` in
  `open_thread_token` is that name's first use in this unit.
- **The six proxies' C declaration lines are carried as `/// C:` doc lines** (`/// C: static BOOL
  WINAPI`, `/// C: process32_first (HANDLE hSnapshot, LPPROCESSENTRY32 lppe)`) rather than with the
  `// C nnn` marker the port's own lines carry: C's *declaration* has no Rust counterpart line, so
  the doc comment is where its text lives.  The frozen probe measured what carrying them verbatim
  costs (six unclosed blocks) - the device is not cosmetic.
- **`w32_copy_file`'s `switch`** is the slice's `macro-body-notes.tsv` row: C 6972-6986 keeps its
  four arms and their order in a `match`, with C's `default:` as `_`.

### The eighteenth slice's deviations (C 7187-7382)

- **`list_system_processes` is a non-`static` definition** (`#[unsafe(no_mangle)] pub unsafe extern
  "C" fn`); the four memory proxies, `enable_privilege` (C 7315's `static int`),
  `restore_privilege` (C 7352) and `ltime` (C 7373's `static Lisp_Object`) are plain
  `unsafe fn`/`unsafe extern "system" fn` as their C storage classes say.
- **C 430's SDK block was upgraded for this slice**: `PROCESSENTRY32` is now spelled as a record
  (C's member names and order, `#[repr(C)]`, P-88, `UNVERIFIED` - `<tlhelp32.h>` is not in the
  reference tree) because C 7296-7305 reads `dwSize` and `th32ProcessID` **by value**, and
  `LPPROCESSENTRY32` now names it (`*mut PROCESSENTRY32`) as C's alias does, instead of the
  pointer-only `*mut c_void` the P-72 ③ rule allowed while the record was only ever passed
  through.  `ULONG_PTR` (the record's fourth member) is declared there as `usize` (`w32term.rs`
  175's spelling, PORTING.md 1.1's handle rule).  The typedef doc line above it now carries the
  SDK's whole record instead of an ellipsis.
- **`priv` is a Rust *reserved* keyword**, so `enable_privilege`'s local and `restore_privilege`'s
  parameter are `r#priv` throughout (the `r#type` device of slice 16; the frozen probe caught it
  on this slice's first run).
- **C 7301-7302's `for (res = process32_first (…); res; res = process32_next (…))`** is the port's
  `while` with the loop-carried assignments (the `w32_cached_id` device), and C 7300's
  `sizeof (PROCESSENTRY32)` is `core::mem::size_of::<PROCESSENTRY32> () as DWORD` (a *type*
  argument, unlike the `size_of_val (&x)` form C's variable-sized `sizeof`s take - C 7319's
  `sizeof (priv)` and C 7320/7355's `sizeof (*old_priv)`/`sizeof (*priv)` are `size_of_val`
  against the value and the pointee).
- **C 7340's `LookupPrivilegeValue (NULL, priv_name, …)` is a `TEXT ()` macro**: the port declares
  and calls the **ANSI** entry point `LookupPrivilegeValueA` (C's `LPCTSTR` is the ANSI form per
  C 284's reading), which is why `docs/ports/token-aliases.tsv` records
  `lookupprivilegevalue,lookupprivilegevaluea` as one family; the `unicode` arm is owed with the
  `SE_*_NAME` constants.  `AdjustTokenPrivileges` (no `TEXT` alias) and `GetCurrentThread` keep
  their unsuffixed names, and `NULL` arguments are `std::ptr::null ()` (the `LPCSTR` first
  parameter) or `std::ptr::null_mut ()` (the two `PreviousState`/`ReturnLength` pointers).
- **The names this slice first uses are declared in-unit** (P-88, `UNVERIFIED`):
  `<tlhelp32.h>`'s `TH32CS_SNAPPROCESS 0x2`, `<winnt.h>`'s `TOKEN_ADJUST_PRIVILEGES 0x0020` and
  `SE_PRIVILEGE_ENABLED 0x2` with `SecurityImpersonation` (the `SECURITY_IMPERSONATION_LEVEL`
  member C 7331 names; the type is C 457's `c_int` alias), `<winerror.h>`'s `ERROR_NO_TOKEN 1008`,
  and the three `<securitybaseapi.h>` entry points `AdjustTokenPrivileges`,
  `LookupPrivilegeValueA` and `GetCurrentThread`.
- **`ltime`'s arithmetic keeps C's types and casts at the call**: `time_sec`/`time_100ns` are
  `ULONGLONG` and `subsec` is C's `int`, and the four `list4i` arguments are cast `as EMACS_INT`
  (the `INT_TO_INTEGER` call-site convention `dbusbind.rs` uses).  C 7305's
  `Fcons (INT_TO_INTEGER (proc_id), proclist)` casts `proc_id` the same way.
- **`U64_TO_LISP_TIME` is a function-like macro**, so it is a `macro_rules!` (PORTING.md 2.1)
  carrying C's body verbatim and gated on `windowsnt` like the unit's other macros; it is the
  third of the four `missing macro` names (the other three are Owed 6's).

### The nineteenth slice's deviations (C 7383-7716)

- **`system_process_attributes` is a non-`static` definition**
  (`#[unsafe(no_mangle)] pub unsafe extern "C" fn`); `process_times` is C's `static int` and stays
  a plain `unsafe fn`.
- **Two more SDK records are spelled, both because C holds them by value**: C 7453's
  `PROCESS_MEMORY_COUNTERS` (the C 420-440 block now carries the record and its alias points at it,
  as `PROCESSENTRY32`'s did in slice 18) and C 7454's `PROCESS_MEMORY_COUNTERS_EX`, whose name C
  *does* spell - but only in the older-SDK arm of C 140-159 (`_WIN32_WINNT < 0x0500` and the old
  `psapi.h`).  The port already carried that arm; this slice adds the SDK arm's twin under C's own
  condition's complement (`not (all (win32_winnt_lt_0500, mingw32_w32api_lt_3_15))`) so the two
  copies never coexist (PORTING.md's one-definition-per-arm rule) - the first *complementary* gate
  of this lane (the earlier ones were one-sided).  Both records are `UNVERIFIED` (`<psapi.h>` is
  not in the reference tree).
- **C 7519's `enable_privilege (SE_DEBUG_NAME, TRUE, &priv_current)`** is the `&CStr` device
  (`.as_ptr ()`) of slice 14/16/18; `SE_DEBUG_NAME` is declared in-unit with its two siblings' owed
  `unicode` arm, and `PROCESS_QUERY_INFORMATION` joins C 1528's `PROCESS_VM_READ`.
- **C 7485's `build_unibyte_string (pe.szExeFile)`** is the record member's address
  (`pe.szExeFile.as_ptr ()`), C 7464's `FLOATP (pid) ? XFLOAT_DATA (pid) : XFIXNUM (pid)` is Rust's
  `if FLOATP (pid) { XFLOAT_DATA (pid) as DWORD } else { XFIXNUM (pid) as DWORD }` (`FLOATP` returns
  a `bool` here, so no `!= 0`), every `INT_TO_INTEGER` call casts to `EMACS_INT` (the `dbusbind.rs`
  convention) and `make_fixnum (pe.pcPriClassBase)` casts the same way.
- **C 7575's `xrealloc (buf, blen = needed)`** keeps C's assignment inside the argument as a Rust
  block - `xrealloc (buf as *mut c_void, { blen = needed; blen } as usize) as *mut c_uchar` - the
  same spelling this unit's C 2321 already uses (measured: that precedent is in the same file).
- **C 7532/7537/7571/7577's `(LPVOID)buf` casts** are `buf as LPVOID` (the C 2320 shape), C
  7556/7616/7617's `strlen`-into-`DWORD` stores are `as DWORD`, and C 7540/7581's
  `memcpy (&user_token, buf, sizeof (user_token))` is the `&raw mut`-plus-`size_of_val` form this
  unit's SEH section already uses.
- **The `double` arithmetic keeps C's shape with explicit casts**: C 7424's
  `100.0 * (tem1 + tem2) / tem` is `100.0 * ((tem1 + tem2) as c_double) / (tem as c_double)`, C
  7659/7661's `ullTotalPhys / 1024.0` is `as c_double / 1024.0`, C 7677/7678's
  `100. * rss / totphys` is `100.0 * rss as c_double / totphys`, and `if (totphys)` is
  `totphys != 0.0` (a `double` truth test, not `!= 0`).
- **C 7682 reads `mem_ex.WorkingSetSize` inside the `get_process_memory_info (h_proc, &mem, …)`
  arm** - C's own text (the second branch's `rss` comes from the *extended* record while the
  attributes come from the plain one).  The port copies that verbatim, with a marker saying so;
  it is C's behaviour, not a transcription slip.
- **C 7711's `/* FIXME: Retrieve command line by walking the PEB of the process.  */`** is carried
  as C's own text, and C 7474-7475's two-assignment `for` is the `while` device of slice 18 (the
  `break` at C 7501 is Rust's `break`).

### The twentieth slice's deviations (C 7717-7806)

- **`w32_memory_info` is a non-`static` definition** with `unsigned long long *` parameters
  (`*mut c_ulonglong`, PORTING.md 1.1's mapping); the thirty-three `pfn_*` statics are C's
  `static … (PASCAL *…) (…)` and become `static mut pfn_X: Option<unsafe extern "system" fn (…) ->
  …> = None;` - C's `PASCAL` is `__stdcall`, i.e. Rust's `"system"`, and the `*_Proc` typedefs
  above already spell that (the `pfn_SetHandleInformation` one is `WINAPI`, the same convention).
- **The socket types are declared in-unit** (P-88, `UNVERIFIED`) because
  `femacs/nt/inc/sys/include/socket.rs` says so itself: it spells `u_short`, `u_long`,
  `struct sockaddr`, `struct hostent`, `struct servent` and `struct addrinfo` the way C does and
  assigns them to the including unit (Owed 9).  Added here: `WORD` is *not* redeclared (this unit's
  SDK type block already has it - the first arm run measured the duplicate as an `E0428`),
  `SOCKET = usize` (`UINT_PTR`), `u_short = c_ushort`, `u_long = u32` (the Windows 32-bit
  `unsigned long`, PORTING.md 1.1's width rule - not Rust's 64-bit `c_ulong`), the four records
  (`sockaddr` with `sa_family`/`sa_data[14]`, `hostent`, `servent`, `addrinfo`), the winsock
  `WSADATA` (C 7837 holds one by value next slice) with `LPWSADATA`, and `WSANETWORKEVENTS` with
  its `FD_MAX_EVENTS` 10 array.  `ws_fd_set` - the one name the same mirror leaves to its includer
  that this slice does not need - is deliberately left for the slice that reads it.
- **C 7804-7806's `#ifndef HANDLE_FLAG_INHERIT`** is the constant device (the `SDDL_REVISION_1`
  shape) and was this file's last `missing constant` row.
- **The thirty-three `pfn_*` C declarations are carried as `/// C:` doc lines** (the
  slice-17/18/19 device for carried *code* text).  C wraps six of them over two lines; those are
  emitted with **both** C lines in the doc run *before* the declaration, because a `///` line
  inside a parameter list is `documentation comments cannot be applied to function parameters`
  (measured by this slice's first arm run) - the declaration itself is one Rust statement.
- **`c_longlong` is added to `include/lisp.rs`'s `core::ffi` import list**: the `nt/inc` mirrors
  spell MSVC's `__int64` that way (`sys/stat.rs`'s `st_size`, `resource.rs`'s `rlim_t`) and every
  unit including `lisp.rs` shares the `use`, so the three `cannot find type c_longlong` rows this
  file has carried since slice 1 retire with it (the mirror's own ledger records the change).
- **C 7725-7726's two-line comment** and C 7749-7756's banner are carried verbatim, and C 7724/
  7736's `sizeof (memstex)`/`sizeof (memst)` are the `size_of_val` form (variables, not types).

### The twenty-first slice's deviations (C 7807-7927)

- **C 7808-7809's `extern HANDLE winsock_lib;` / `HANDLE winsock_lib;` pair is one item**
  (PORTING.md 0.1-12) and C spells the definition without `static`, so it is
  `#[unsafe(no_mangle)] pub static mut winsock_lib: HANDLE = std::ptr::null_mut ();` (the C 592/597
  device); `winsock_inuse` is C's tentative `static int`, i.e. `static mut winsock_inuse: c_int =
  0;`.  C 7812's `BOOL term_winsock (void);` has no Rust item (Rust resolves forward references),
  so its line is carried in the slice's comment - the C 800-812 device.
