- **C 7852-7854's `LOAD_PROC` macro is a `macro_rules!` whose label is a *parameter***:
  `($label:lifetime, $pfn:ident, $name:expr) => { … if $pfn.is_none () { break $label; } }`, and the
  thirty call sites pass `'fail` themselves.  That is not cosmetic: `macro_rules!` hygiene does not
  let a `'fail` written *inside* the macro body see the caller's label, and the first arm run of
  this slice measured exactly that (**31 `E0426: use of undeclared label 'fail` sites** - the
  thirty invocations plus C 7903's own jump).  C's `##fn`/`#fn` pasting has no Rust spelling, so
  each call site passes the two pasted names (`pfn_WSAStartup`, `c"WSAStartup"`) - the
  `LOAD_DLL_FN!` device of `decompress.rs` 237, whose doc comment records the same rule.
- **C 7921's `fail:` label is a labelled block**: `'fail: { … }` whose *tail* is C's label body
  (`FreeLibrary (winsock_lib); winsock_lib = NULL;`).  C reaches it three ways - the thirty
  `LOAD_PROC` gotos, C 7903's `goto fail`, and the fall-through of C 7900's `if` when
  `pfn_WSAStartup` returns non-zero - and the port reproduces all three (`break 'fail` for the
  jumps, the block's natural end for the fall-through).  The two `pfn_*` loads that C writes out
  (C 7890-7891) are `std::mem::transmute::<VOIDFNPTR, _> (get_proc_addr (…))` like the macro's, and
  C 7894-7897's `== NULL` coupling is `.is_none ()`.
- **The two names this slice first uses are declared in-unit** (P-88, `UNVERIFIED`):
  `<winsock2.h>`'s `WSAENETDOWN 10050` - which the in-scope
  `femacs/nt/inc/sys/include/socket.rs` 135 already *uses* for its `ENETDOWN` row while saying
  "the `WSA*` names come from `winsock2.h`, which has no mirror" - and `<kernel32.h>`'s
  `FreeLibrary`.  `release_listen_threads` (C 7819) needs nothing: `src/w32.h` 173 declares it and
  that mirror is in this unit's include graph.
- **C's pointer-vs-value spellings are kept**: `winsock_lib` is compared with `.is_null ()` (C's
  `!= NULL`/`== NULL`), the two `winsock_lib = NULL;` stores are `std::ptr::null_mut ()`, and
  `winsockData` (C's uninitialised `WSADATA` local, PORTING.md 1.5) is zeroed.  `load_now` needs
  no `mut` (C only reads it), and C 7850's banner comment is carried verbatim.

### The twenty-second slice's deviations (C 7928-7968)

- **C 7930's `int h_errno = 0;` is the definition of a name the mirror already declares**:
  `femacs/nt/inc/sys/include/socket.rs` 232 carries `pub static mut h_errno: c_int;` (its C 139
  `extern int h_errno;`), so the port writes the definition itself -
  `#[unsafe(no_mangle)] pub static mut h_errno: c_int = 0;` - and the definition shadows the
  glob-imported declaration (PORTING.md 0.1-10, the `careadlinkat` device).  C spells no `static`,
  so the symbol is exported (the `winsock_lib` device), and the arm probe adds no `E0428` for it.
- **C 7946-7959's `switch (wsa_err)` is a `match`**: C's ten labels in C's order, C's `default:` as
  the `_` arm holding `wsa_err` (PORTING.md 2.2 - the `stat_worker`/`readlink`/`w32_copy_file`
  family).  C's eleven `break;` statements have no counterpart after a `match` arm, C's un-braced
  single-statement bodies (C 7942/7944, C 7967) get the braces Rust requires (the
  slice-17/18/19/20 device), and C's `errno = …` stores are `*errno () = …;` (the `errno ()` reader
  of `lib/include/errno.rs` 17, this file's C 810 spelling).
- **The ten `WSAE*` values and the CRT's `ENOTEMPTY` are declared in-unit** (P-88, `UNVERIFIED` -
  Owed 24): `<winsock2.h>` has no mirror, and `femacs/nt/inc/sys/include/socket.rs` 103 says exactly
  that in its own note while its 105/149 rows *use* two of the ten (`EWOULDBLOCK = WSAEWOULDBLOCK`,
  `ENOTCONN = WSAENOTCONN`) - which is why this slice's arm reading retires those two rows.
  `ENOTEMPTY` is the CRT's `<errno.h>`: the tree's `nt/inc/sys/socket.h` 263 keeps its
  `WSAENOTEMPTY` alias commented out and the `lib/include/errno.rs` mirror does not carry the name,
  so the value (41) is a decision recorded here, not a measurement.
- **C's two `static void` helpers stay non-`pub`**: `unsafe fn set_errno ()` and
  `unsafe fn check_errno ()`, each under `#[cfg (feature = "windowsnt")]`.  C 7935-7936 and
  7962-7963 (the two-line `static void` + name) are one Rust item each, so the marker sits on C 7935
  and C 7962 (the `term_winsock` device), and `pfn_WSAGetLastError ()`/`pfn_WSASetLastError (0)` are
  the `Option`-typed statics of slice 20, called through `.unwrap () (…)` (C's call-through).

### The twenty-third slice's deviations (C 7969-8061)

- **C's anonymous element type takes the variable's name** (P-67): `pub struct _wsa_errlist {
  pub errnum: c_int, pub msg: *const c_char }` with `static mut _wsa_errlist: [_wsa_errlist; …]`,
  which Rust's separate type and value namespaces allow - the `w16select.rs` 268-280 device for the
  same `static struct { … }` idiom (C has neither a tag nor a typedef here).  C's thirteen
  `/* not sure */` row comments are carried before the `// C NNNN` marker and C's four initializer
  blanks stay as formatting.
- **The rows are C's own bytes**: each `{NAME, "message"}` becomes
  `_wsa_errlist { errnum: NAME, msg: c"message".as_ptr () },` - the messages were parsed out of the
  C text rather than retyped - and C 8043's `{-1, NULL}` sentinel is `std::ptr::null ()` (the
  file's `NULL` spelling for a `*const` field).
- **C 8024-8036's `#ifdef WSAENOMORE` arm is the `wsaenomore` feature, and the array length is
  spelled with the same feature**:
  `[_wsa_errlist; if cfg! (feature = "wsaenomore") { 63 } else { 52 }]`.  C's guard is an SDK
  predicate: `<winsock2.h>` defines that family from `_WIN32_WINNT >= 0x0600` on, and C 141-143
  *leaves `_WIN32_WINNT` alone* under `MINGW_W64` ("MinGW64 defines this to a higher value in its
  system headers, and is not really compatible with values lower than 0x0500") while the
  non-`MINGW_W64` arm pins it to 0x0400 - so both arms are real configurations (the C 140/141
  `win32_winnt_lt_0500`/`mingw_w64` device), and the length, which Rust cannot infer in a `static`,
  has to be written per arm.  The eleven rows and their eleven constants sit under the feature; the
  SDK's own `0x0600` requirement is `UNVERIFIED` (Owed 25).
- **Fifty-one `WSA*` names are declared in-unit** (P-88, `UNVERIFIED`): the
  `femacs/nt/inc/sys/include/socket.rs` mirror's 103 note says the `WSA*` names come from
  `<winsock2.h>`, "which has no mirror", while its own rows 106-172 *use* thirty-two of them for
  the `E*` aliases - so those names belong to the including unit, exactly as `WSAENETDOWN` did in
  slice 21 and the ten `set_errno` names in slice 22.  After this slice the mirror's remaining
  unresolved rows are two: `ws_fd_set` (its own type) and `O_NOINHERIT` (`<fcntl.h>`'s, which its
  95 row `O_CLOEXEC` depends on - a `lib/include/fcntl.in.rs` row, Owed 9).
- **`sys_strerror` is this lane's next exported definition and it shadows the mirror's
  declaration**: `ms-w32.h` 273 declares `char *sys_strerror (int);`, the mirror carries it as
  `ms_w32_decls`' `pub fn sys_strerror` (303-304) and glob-imports that module, and a *definition*
  shadows a glob-imported declaration (PORTING.md 0.1-10, the `careadlinkat` device) - the arm
  reading adds no `E0428`.  Its read of the CRT's table needs two in-unit declarations: `_sys_nerr`
  (the name the mirror's `sys_nerr! ()` macro body uses; ms-w32.h 460-462's rename) and
  `sys_errlist` (C's own spelling - ms-w32.h renames `sys_nerr` only, so the CRT's own name is
  `UNVERIFIED`; the unknown-length device is `[*mut c_char; 0]`, the `unistd.in.rs` 120 `environ`
  shape).  C's `for (i = 0; _wsa_errlist[i].errnum >= 0; i++)` is `i = 0;` plus `while …` plus
  `i += 1;` (the file's device, C's increment inside), C's `(char *)` cast is `as *mut c_char`,
  C's array decay into `sprintf` is `.as_mut_ptr ()`, and C's function-`static`
  `char unknown_msg[40]` is a `static mut` inside the function.

### The twenty-fourth slice's deviations (C 8062-8092)

- **C 8067's `SOCK_HANDLE` is a `macro_rules!`**: `($fd:expr) => { (fd_info[$fd as usize].hnd)
  as SOCKET }` - C's cast is Rust's `as SOCKET` and C's `fd_info[fd]` index needs `as usize`
  (`fd` is an `int`).  `fd_info` is this unit's own object (C 3300; `femacs/src/w32.rs` 5355) and
  `hnd` its `HANDLE` member (`include/w32.rs` 227).  Retiring this macro empties the file's
  `missing macro` list.
- **`sys_socket` is exported and keeps C's two early exits**: `#[unsafe(no_mangle)] pub unsafe
  extern "C" fn sys_socket (af: c_int, r#type: c_int, protocol: c_int) -> c_int` - C's second
  parameter is spelled `type`, a Rust keyword, so the slice-16 `r#type` device applies - C's
  `errno = ENETDOWN` is `*errno () = ENETDOWN;` (the file's `errno ()` reader), and C's
  `s != INVALID_SOCKET` is its own unit-declared constant.
- **`INVALID_SOCKET` is declared in-unit** (P-88, `UNVERIFIED`): `<winsock2.h>`'s `(SOCKET) (~0)`
  becomes `pub const INVALID_SOCKET: SOCKET = -1isize as SOCKET;` - the C 1540
  `INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE` spelling (`femacs/src/w32.rs` 1886).  The socket mirror declares neither
  `SOCKET` (this unit declares it, C 7762-7768's block) nor `INVALID_SOCKET`, so the name is the
  including unit's (Owed 26).
- **C's call-through and C's widening are one line**: `s = pfn_socket.unwrap () (af, r#type,
  protocol) as SOCKET;` - the `Option`-typed static of slice 20 called through, and the `int`
  result C assigns to a `SOCKET` spelled out, since Rust rejects C's implicit conversion (the
  `pfn_WSAGetLastError`/`set_errno` family of conversions in this file).  The `SOCKET s` local
  follows the C 7290 `h_snapshot` device - declared, then zeroed under
  `/* C leaves `s` uninitialised (PORTING.md 1.5).  */`.

### The twenty-fifth slice's deviations (C 8093-8188)

- **`child_process` and the three `<windows.h>` records are spelled by this unit**, because
  `include/w32.rs`'s note 34-42 *defers* them (C 100's `PROCESS_INFORMATION` and C 110-111's two
  `OVERLAPPED` are members **by value**, and `<windows.h>` has no mirror): `PROCESS_INFORMATION`
  (24 bytes), `OVERLAPPED` (32 bytes, whose anonymous union and struct take container-derived
  names per P-67 - `OVERLAPPED_inner`/`OVERLAPPED_offset`) and `_child_process` itself (C's eleven
  members, in C's order, `status`/`errcode` plain `c_int` since C's `volatile` has no ABI effect
  here) under `#[cfg (feature = "windowsnt")]`, with the `child_process` typedef beside it.  The
  mirror's own copy of the record sits under `feature = "windows"` (the Cygwin arm of that gate,
  whose cycle the mirror's note 21-24 records), so the two never meet in the arms the port models
  (`--cfg 'feature="windowsnt"'`, P-86 ③⑵) - a build that turned both on would collide, and
  Owed 27 records it.  C's per-member prose (w32.h 76-112) is not carried, as in the mirror's own
  arm; C's member declarations are cited in the `/// C:` lines.
- **C 8121's truth test and C 8128's initial value**: `if (pfn_SetHandleInformation)` is
  `pfn_SetHandleInformation.is_some ()` (the `null` family's `Option` spelling, Owed 18's note),
  the call is the `.unwrap () (…)` call-through of slice 20's `*_Proc` statics, and C's
  `HANDLE new_s = INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE;` is this unit's constant (C 1540's declaration).
- **The four names this slice first uses are declared in-unit** (P-88, `UNVERIFIED`): `<winbase.h>`'s
  `DUPLICATE_SAME_ACCESS` (`0x00000002`) and its `DuplicateHandle` prototype (an `extern "system"`
  declaration beside `FreeLibrary`'s, slice 21), `<winsock2.h>`'s `FIONBIO`
  (`0x8004667e`; it is `_IOW ('f', 126, u_long)` in that SDK, and no header in the reference tree
  defines it), and the CRT's `_O_RDWR` (`0x0002`) - the underscore spelling, which is not
  `lib/include/fcntl.in.rs`'s gnulib `O_RDWR` (Owed 13's divergence note).  `_close` is declared
  beside the unit's own `_open` (line 6728): the `lib/include/unistd.in.rs` copy of the name is its
  `windows`-arm gnulib rename (`close` → `_close`), so for this arm the CRT's `<io.h>` declaration
  is the unit's.
- **C's cast and call sites keep their spellings**: `s as HANDLE` (C 8156/8123/8131), `new_s as
  SOCKET` (C 8146), `(SOCKET) new_s` inside C 8143's call is `new_s as SOCKET`, C 8159's four-flag
  set is `(FILE_SOCKET | FILE_BINARY | FILE_READ | FILE_WRITE) as c_uint` (the mirror's `FILE_*`
  are `c_int`, `filedesc.flags` is `c_uint`), C 8161's `new_child ()` returns the mirror's
  `*mut c_void` and the unit writes `as *mut child_process`, and the three `fd_info[fd]` sites are
  `fd_info[fd as usize]` (the C 2887 device).  C 8170's `DebPrint ((…))` is the `DebPrint!` macro
  of the `conf_post.rs` mirror (the C 2400 site's spelling) and `winsock_inuse++` is
  `winsock_inuse += 1;` (the file's increment device).  C 8184-8185's un-braced `else` body gets
  Rust's braces.

### The twenty-sixth slice's deviations (C 8189-8240)

- **`SOCKET_ERROR` is declared in-unit** (P-88, `UNVERIFIED`): `<winsock2.h>`'s `#define SOCKET_ERROR
  (-1)`, written `pub const SOCKET_ERROR: c_int = -1;` (the file's `c_int` return convention; C's
  own prototype spells it `int`).  It joins Owed 24-27's Windows-host pass.
- **C's `flags & FILE_SOCKET` needs a written conversion**: `filedesc.flags` is `c_uint` (the
  mirror's C 120-124 reading) while the `FILE_*` flags are `c_int`, so C 8200/8221's test is
  `(fd_info[s as usize].flags & FILE_SOCKET as c_uint) != 0` and C 8233's store is
  `fd_info[s as usize].flags |= FILE_CONNECT as c_uint;` - the same written-out pair C 8159 needed
  in slice 25.  The index is `fd_info[s as usize]` (C's `fd_info[s]`).
- **`SOCK_HANDLE` at the call sites**: C 8202/8223 pass `SOCK_HANDLE (s)` into the two `pfn_*`
  statics, so the port writes `pfn_bind.unwrap () (SOCK_HANDLE! (s), addr, namelen)` - the macro
  expansion's `*mut c_void`-to-`SOCKET` cast and the call-through are the same device as slice 24's.
  C's two `errno` stores are `*errno () = …;` and C 8230's `errno == EWOULDBLOCK` is
  `*errno () == EWOULDBLOCK`.
- **C's `? :` and its un-braced bodies**: this slice has none of the four; instead C 8204's
  un-braced `if` body gets Rust's braces (`{ set_errno (); }`) and C 8232's trailing comment is
  carried verbatim after the statement.

### The twenty-seventh slice's deviations (C 8241-8274)

- **C's five ternaries are `return if … { … } else { … };`** - the device C 6619's site uses, whose
  marker names C's ternary - because C's `?`/`:` has no Rust operator and the two arms are the two
  branches C writes on one line (C 8246/8253/8259/8266/8273).  C's `winsock_lib != NULL` is
  `! winsock_lib.is_null ()` and C's `pfn_X (arg)` is the `.unwrap () (…)` call-through (slice 20's
  `*_Proc` statics, P-27).
- **`INADDR_NONE` is declared in-unit** (P-88, `UNVERIFIED`): `<winsock2.h>`'s `#define INADDR_NONE
  0xffffffff`, written `pub const INADDR_NONE: c_ulong = 0xffffffff;` - the `c_ulong` spelling
  slice 20's `pfn_inet_addr` return already carries for C's `unsigned long`, so the ternary's two
  arms have one Rust type.  It joins Owed 24-28's Windows-host pass.
- **The five definitions are exported** (`#[unsafe(no_mangle)] pub unsafe extern "C" fn`): C spells
  them without `static`, and `w32.h`'s own declarations of them are... none (the header declares
  only the process/fd entry points), so nothing shadows the definitions.

### The twenty-eighth slice's deviations (C 8275-8308)

- **`MAX_COMPUTERNAME_LENGTH` and `GetComputerNameA` are declared in-unit** (P-88, `UNVERIFIED`):
  `<winbase.h>`'s `#define MAX_COMPUTERNAME_LENGTH 15` (written `c_int`, C 8292 compares it against
  `sizeof`, so the port writes `MAX_COMPUTERNAME_LENGTH as usize`) and the `WINBASEAPI BOOL WINAPI
  GetComputerNameA (LPSTR, LPDWORD);` prototype as an `extern "system"` declaration beside the
  other kernel32 names this file declares.
- **`sizeof` is the `size_of_val` arm of its token family**: C 8288's `sizeof (sname)` is
  `core::mem::size_of_val (&sname) as c_int` (the call's second parameter is C's `int`, so the
  `size_t` is written out) and C 8292's is `core::mem::size_of_val (&sname) > MAX_COMPUTERNAME_LENGTH
  as usize`; C 8303's `char hostname[MAX_UTF8_PATH]` is `[c_char; MAX_UTF8_PATH as usize]` (the C
  815/836 device) and C's `sname[256+1]` is `[c_char; 256 + 1]` (C 7442's spelling).
- **C's `!` and its cast**: `retval = !GetComputerNameA (sname, (DWORD *)&nlen);` is
  `retval = (GetComputerNameA (sname.as_mut_ptr (), &raw mut nlen as *mut DWORD) == 0) as c_int;` -
  the boolean C stores becomes an `int` (the `retval` store's type) and C's `(DWORD *)` cast is
  `&raw mut nlen as *mut DWORD` (the C 576 device).  C's `name[namelen] = '\0'` is
  `*name.offset (namelen as isize) = 0;` (the C 2813 site's spelling), C's array arguments decay the
  way C's do (`sname.as_mut_ptr ()`), C 8290's un-braced `if` body gets Rust's braces, and
  `retval` follows the C 7290 `h_snapshot` device (declared, then zeroed under a
  `/* C leaves … uninitialised (PORTING.md 1.5).  */` line).

### The twenty-ninth slice's deviations (C 8309-8351)

- **`NO_RECOVERY` is declared in-unit** (P-88, `UNVERIFIED`): `<netdb.h>`'s `#define NO_RECOVERY 3`
  (the tree's own `emacs/nt/inc/netdb.h` does not define it, and the port's `include/netdb.rs`
  mirror carries other names), written `pub const NO_RECOVERY: c_int = 3;`.  It joins Owed 24-30's
  Windows-host pass.
- **`h_errno` is written directly**: C 8314/8318/8328/8331 reach the object this unit defines
  (slice 22's C 7930), so the port reads `let mut h_err: c_int = h_errno;`, stores
  `h_errno = NO_RECOVERY;`/`h_errno = *errno ();` and restores `h_errno = h_err;` - C's `errno`
  macro read is `*errno ()` (the C 810 spelling).
- **C's `!host`/`!serv` tests and its `NULL` returns**: `if host.is_null ()`/`if serv.is_null ()`
  and `return std::ptr::null_mut ();` (the file's `NULL` spelling for a pointer result); the two
  locals follow the C 7290 `h_snapshot` device (declared, then nulled under
  `/* C leaves … uninitialised (PORTING.md 1.5).  */`), C 8349's un-braced `if` body gets Rust's
  braces, and both definitions are exported (`#[unsafe(no_mangle)]`, C spelling them without
  `static`).

### The thirtieth slice's deviations (C 8352-8372)

- **Nothing new is spelled**: `sys_getpeername` is slice 26's shape with C's own three parameters
  (`s`, `addr`, `namelen`), so the only written forms are the two conversions those slices already
  established - `fd_info[s as usize].flags & FILE_SOCKET as c_uint` (the `c_uint` field against the
  `c_int` flag) and `SOCK_HANDLE! (s)` at the call site - plus C 8367's un-braced `if` body getting
  Rust's braces.  C's `addr`/`namelen` pass through unchanged (`*mut sockaddr`/`*mut c_int`, the
  `pfn_getpeername` declaration's own types, C 7770's block).

### The thirty-first slice's deviations (C 8373-8464)

- **`gai_storage` is declared where C declares it**: a block-scope `#[repr(C)] struct gai_storage {
  addrinfo: addrinfo, sockaddr_in: sockaddr_in }` inside the function (C 8393-8396 is a nested struct
  definition), named after the variable per P-67 (the slice-25 `_wsa_errlist` device, whose type and
  value namespaces are separate), and its pointer is `*mut gai_storage` from
  `xzalloc (size_of::<gai_storage> ()) as *mut gai_storage` (C 8424's `sizeof *gai_storage`).
- **Four names are declared in-unit, all `UNVERIFIED`** (P-88, Owed 32): `<winsock2.h>`'s
  `SOCK_DGRAM` (2), `in_addr` (4 bytes), `sockaddr_in` (`short sin_family; u_short sin_port;
  struct in_addr sin_addr; char sin_zero[8]` = 16 bytes, C's own member names and order) and the
  block-scope `gai_storage` record.  **Two names this slice *uses* are mirrors'** and are therefore
  not declared here - `strtoul` (`lib/include/stdlib.in.rs`, measured: an in-unit copy is an
  `E0428`) and `xzalloc` (`include/lisp.rs`, measured by `check-mirror-dupes.py`); the call is C's
  `strtoul (service, &endp, 10)` with the `unsigned long`-to-`int` conversion written out.
- **C's `h_addr` is the `<netdb.h>` macro**: C 8430-8431's `host_info->h_addr, host_info->h_length`
  is `*(*host_info).h_addr_list as *const c_void` and `(*host_info).h_length as usize` (the
  `macro-body-notes.tsv` row records it), while C 8433's `sin_family = h_addrtype` passes through
  (`c_short` both sides).
- **C's implicit conversions and ternaries are written out**: `(*srv).s_port as c_int` (C 8411),
  `port as u_short` (C 8419) into `sys_htons` and `as c_int` back into `port` (C 8419),
  `port as u_short` (C 8425), `ai_family as c_short` (C 8447), `sin_family as c_int` (C 8455); the
  four `(hints) ? … : 0` and `(hints && …) ? "udp" : "tcp"` expressions are `if … { … } else { … };`
  (the C 6619 device); `numeric_addr == -1` is `numeric_addr == -1i32 as c_ulong`, because mingw's
  `unsigned long` is 32 bits while the port's `c_ulong` is not (C 8439); `struct sockaddr *` casts
  are `as *mut sockaddr` (C 8450-8451) and the two `memcpy` sources/destinations are `&raw mut`/
  `&raw const` casts to `*mut c_void`/`*const c_void`.  C's `rc`, `host_info`, `gai_storage` and
  `endp` follow the C 7290 `h_snapshot` device (declared, then zeroed under a
  `/* C leaves … uninitialised (PORTING.md 1.5).  */` line).

### The thirty-second slice's deviations (C 8465-8549)

- **Nothing new is spelled**: the four wrappers reuse devices the lane already established -
  `pfn_freeaddrinfo.is_some ()`/`.unwrap () (…)` (C 8476-8477's truth test and call-through, the

- **C's un-braced bodies get Rust's braces** (C 8499/8521/8542/8544), and the four definitions are
  exported (`#[unsafe(no_mangle)] pub unsafe extern "C" fn`) because C spells none of them
  `static`.  `sys_setsockopt`'s five-parameter signature is written across two lines exactly as
  the `pfn_setsockopt` declaration's own parameter list is (C 7787's block).

### The thirty-third slice's deviations (C 8550-8644)

- **`ResetEvent` is declared in-unit** (P-88, `UNVERIFIED`): `<winbase.h>`'s

  the store's `STATUS_READ_ACKNOWLEDGED` is the `w32.h` mirror's enum row (its 201) and the call is
  the file's own `ResetEvent (…)`.
- **The rest is this lane's established spelling**: C 8582's `flags & FILE_LISTEN` is the
  `c_uint`-against-`c_int` form (`FILE_LISTEN as c_uint`), the three `pfn_*` calls are the
  `.unwrap () (…)` call-throughs with `SOCK_HANDLE! (s)`, C's two-line parameter lists are written

  keeps its initial value, C 8587/8617/8639's un-braced bodies get Rust's braces, and all four

### The thirty-fourth slice's deviations (C 8645-8707)

- **`PIPE_NOWAIT` and `SetNamedPipeHandleState` are declared in-unit** (P-88, `UNVERIFIED`):

### The thirty-fifth slice's deviations (C 8708-8821)

- **`STILL_ACTIVE` and `GetExitCodeThread` are declared in-unit** (P-88, `UNVERIFIED`):
  `<winbase.h>`'s `#define STILL_ACTIVE 0x00000103` (written `DWORD`) and the
  `WINBASEAPI BOOL WINAPI GetExitCodeThread (HANDLE, LPDWORD);` prototype as an `extern "system"`
  declaration.  They join Owed 24-34's Windows-host pass.
- **C's `for (i = 0; i < MAXDESC; i++)` with a `continue` is the new device here**: the port writes
  `i = 0; while i < MAXDESC { … }` (the C 2885 device) and, because a `while`'s `continue` would skip
  the increment a `for`'s runs, C 8748-8749's `continue` is spelled `{ i += 1; continue; }` with the
  comment `// C 8748 (C's `continue` plus the `for`'s increment)` - the loop's other two exits keep
  C's shape (`break` at C 8751, the natural `while` exit at `i == MAXDESC`).
- **The record is reached through slice 25's spelling**: `fd_info[fd].cp` is the mirror's
  `*mut c_void`, so the local is `fd_info[fd as usize].cp as *mut child_process` (the `delete_child`
  call casts back with `cp as *mut c_void`), C's `cp->thrd`/`cp->status`/`cp->procinfo.hProcess`/

  device) with the `FILE_*` flags converted `as c_uint`, and C 8757's one-line
  `if (winsock_lib == NULL) emacs_abort ();` stays one line (`{ emacs_abort (); }`).

### The thirty-sixth slice's deviations (C 8822-8875)

- **`_dup` and `_dup2` are declared in-unit** (P-88, `UNVERIFIED`): the CRT's `<io.h>` pair,
  `int _dup (int);` and `int _dup2 (int src, int dst);` (the second returns 0 on success, which is
  what C 8869's test reads).  They join Owed 24-35's Windows-host pass.
- **The two copies are C's own object assignment**: `fd_info[new_fd as usize] = fd_info[fd as usize]`
  (C 8832) and `fd_info[dst as usize] = fd_info[src as usize]` (C 8872) copy the unit's `filedesc`
  record by value - the type is `Copy` because C 3300's definition is a fixed table (`[FILEDESC_INIT;
  MAXDESC as usize]`).  C 8865's `flags != 0` test is the mirror field's `c_uint` against the literal,
  and C 8866's call is slice 35's own `sys_close (dst)` (which also retires a name the file owes
  itself).
- **C's cast and its ternary**: C 8856's `(HANDLE)_get_osfhandle (src)` is
  `_get_osfhandle (src) as HANDLE` (the unit's C 8525 declaration returns `isize`), C 8844/8858's
  `errno` stores are `*errno () = …;`, C 8830's block keeps C's braces, and C 8874's
  `rc == 0 ? dst : rc` is `return if rc == 0 { dst } else { rc };` (the C 6619 device).  C's `rc` and
  `new_fd` follow the C 7290 `h_snapshot` device.

### The thirty-seventh slice's deviations (C 8876-8923)

- **Four names are declared in-unit, all `UNVERIFIED`** (P-88, Owed 37): the CRT's `O_NOINHERIT`
  (`0x0080`) - which the `nt/inc/sys/socket.rs` mirror's 96 row `O_CLOEXEC = O_NOINHERIT` has been
  waiting on, so that mirror's last unresolved row retires with it - `_O_BINARY` (`0x8000`), the
  `_pipe (int *, unsigned int, int)` prototype, and the extern object `w32_pipe_buffer_size`
  (`*mut c_uint`): **C 8887 reads it out of another translation unit** (`src/w32proc.c` 4846/4851
  defines it) and *no header in the reference tree declares it*, so the unit declares the extern and
  this ledger records the finding - `w32proc.rs`'s own ledger owns the definition when that unit
  lands.  `UINT_MAX` is **not** re-spelled: `lib/include/limits.in.rs` 48 owns it (measured - an
  in-unit copy is an `E0428`, the slice-31 `strtoul` lesson), and `_O_NOINHERIT` is this unit's own
  C 6736 constant while `O_BINARY`/`O_CLOEXEC` are the fcntl mirror's.
- **C's pointer arithmetic and its chained store keep their shapes**: C's `phandles[0]`/`phandles[1]`
  are `*phandles.offset (0)`/`*phandles.offset (1)` (the C 2138 device, `as usize` when they index
  `fd_info`), and C 8908's `phandles[0] = phandles[1] = -1;` is two statements in C's own order.
- **C's flags are written out**: C 8914/8917's `flags = FILE_PIPE | …` are
  `(FILE_PIPE | FILE_READ | FILE_BINARY) as c_uint` (the `c_int` flags against the `c_uint` field),
  C 8881's `unsigned flags;` is `let mut flags: c_uint;`, C 8900's two tests compare the `c_int`
  array entries against `MAXDESC` (both `c_int`), C 8909's `errno` store is `*errno () = EMFILE;`,
  and `rc`/`flags` follow the C 7290 `h_snapshot` device (declared, then zeroed under a
  `/* C leaves … uninitialised (PORTING.md 1.5).  */` line).

### The thirty-eighth slice's deviations (C 8924-9035)

- **Seven names are declared in-unit, all `UNVERIFIED`** (P-88, Owed 38): `<winbase.h>`'s
  `COMMTIMEOUTS` (C's five `DWORD` members in C's order, 20 bytes), its
  `GetCommTimeouts`/`SetCommTimeouts`/`ReadFile`/`GetOverlappedResult` prototypes (each with C's own
  parameter list), `<winerror.h>`'s `ERROR_IO_PENDING` (997) and the CRT's
  `_read (int, void *, unsigned int)`.  The record `OVERLAPPED` is **not** re-spelled - slice 25's
  own spelling is in scope and C 8981 takes the address of the `child_process` member that holds it.
- **C 8972's `while (++wait <= 0)` is the pre-increment device**: the port writes
  `loop { wait += 1; if ! (wait <= 0) { break; } … Sleep (0); }` (the `++wait` runs before each
  test, which is what C's condition means), with C 8973-8974's comment carried inside the loop body
  and the outer `if wait > 0 { Sleep (wait as DWORD); }` keeping C's `int`-to-`DWORD` conversion.
- **C's `sizeof (char)` and its pointer casts are written out**: the three sizes are
  `core::mem::size_of::<c_char> ()` (as `c_uint` for `_read`, as `DWORD` for `ReadFile`, as `c_int`
  for the `pfn_recv` call and C 9029's test), C 8999/9006's `(DWORD *) &rc` are
  `(&raw mut rc) as *mut DWORD`, the `LPVOID` arguments are `&raw mut (*cp).chr as *mut c_void`, and
  C's `ct` (uninitialised in C) follows the C 7290 `h_snapshot` device (declared, then zeroed
  field-by-field under a `/* C leaves … uninitialised (PORTING.md 1.5).  */` line).  `fd_info[fd]`
  sites keep this file's `as usize` and the `FILE_*` flags keep their `as c_uint` pair, while
  `nblock` is C's `unsigned long` (`c_ulong`, the C 8668 spelling) and the definition is exported
  (`#[unsafe(no_mangle)]`, C spelling `_sys_read_ahead` without `static`).

### The thirty-ninth slice's deviations (C 9036-9125)

- **Six names are declared in-unit, all `UNVERIFIED`** (P-88, Owed 39): `<winsock2.h>`'s
  `FD_ACCEPT` (8) and `FD_CONNECT` (16) - typed `c_long`, because that is what the slice-20
  `pfn_WSAEventSelect` declaration takes - plus `FD_CONNECT_BIT` (4), typed `usize` because C uses the
  int constant as an `iErrorCode` index and the mirror's array is `[c_int; FD_MAX_EVENTS as usize]`;
  and `<winbase.h>`'s `WaitForSingleObject` prototype with `WAIT_TIMEOUT` (`0x102`) and
  `WAIT_OBJECT_0` (`0`).  `WSANETWORKEVENTS` is **not** re-spelled - slice 20's record is in scope.
- **C's two `do { … } while (…)` loops are the P-95 device**: `loop { rc = WaitForSingleObject (hEv,
  500); Sleep (5); if ! (rc == WAIT_TIMEOUT && (*cp).status != STATUS_READ_ERROR &&
  ! (*cp).char_avail.is_null ()) { break; } }` - C's pointer truth test `cp->char_avail` becomes
  `! ….is_null ()`, and the condition is tested after the body exactly as C's `do` does.
- **C's `NULL` and its uninitialised locals**: C 9064/9119's deselection passes `std::ptr::null_mut ()`
  (the `pfn`'s `HANDLE` parameter), the record locals `events`/`ct` follow the C 7290 `h_snapshot`
  device (declared, then zeroed - for `events` field by field with
  `iErrorCode: [0; FD_MAX_EVENTS as usize]`), C 9108's truth test on the error code is
  `events.iErrorCode[FD_CONNECT_BIT] != 0`, and both definitions are exported
  (`#[unsafe(no_mangle)]`, C spelling neither `static`).

### The fortieth slice's deviations (C 9126-9347)

- **Three names are declared in-unit, all `UNVERIFIED`** (P-88, Owed 40): `<winbase.h>`'s
  `PeekNamedPipe (HANDLE, LPVOID, DWORD, LPDWORD, LPDWORD, LPOVERLAPPED)`, `MAXDWORD` (`0xFFFFFFFF`)
  and `<winsock2.h>`'s `FIONREAD` (`0x4004667F`, typed `c_long` because that is what the slice-20
  `pfn_ioctlsocket` declaration takes).  Everything else in the body is already in scope: the
  `FILE_*` flags and `FILE_LAST_CR`/`FILE_AT_EOF` are the `w32.h` mirror's rows (254/255), `crlf_to_lf`
  this unit's own C 2537 definition, `ResetEvent`/`COMMTIMEOUTS`/`ReadFile`/`ERROR_IO_PENDING`/
  `GetOverlappedResult` slices 33/38's and the `pfn_*` statics slice 20's.
- **C's `switch (current_status)` is the port's `match`**: C's shared labels become `|`-arms in C's own
  order, C 9197's fall-through into C 9205-9206 exists because a `match` arm simply ends where C's
  `break;` would, C's `default:` is the `_` arm, and C 9183-9193's `#if 0` block is carried as C's own
  text (the port does not expand it) - the `macro-body-notes.tsv` row records all four missing tokens.
- **C's post-increments, casts and conversions are written out**: `*buffer++ = …` is the pair
  `*buffer = …; buffer = buffer.offset (1);` (the C 6925 device, twice), `count--`/`nchars++`/`nchars--`
  are `-= 1`/`+= 1`, `&= ~FILE_LAST_CR` is `&= ! (FILE_LAST_CR as c_uint)`, C 9218's `min (waiting,
  (DWORD) count)` is `core::cmp::min (waiting, count as DWORD) as c_int` (C's implicit narrowing kept),
  the `(HANDLE)`/`(DWORD *)` casts are `as HANDLE`/`(&raw mut rc) as *mut DWORD`, C 9336's
  `orig_buffer[nchars - 1] == 0x0d` is `*orig_buffer.offset ((nchars - 1) as isize) == 0x0d`, the three
  `NULL`s of C 9217 are `std::ptr::null_mut ()`, C's one-line `if`s (C 9156, C 9270) stay one line, and
  `nchars`/`to_read`/`waiting` follow the C 7290 `h_snapshot` device while `orig_buffer` keeps C's
  initial value.  The definition is exported (`#[unsafe(no_mangle)]`, C spelling `sys_read` without
  `static`).

### The forty-first slice's deviations (C 9348-9350)

- **`interrupt_handle` is an in-unit extern, not a definition**: C 9350 declares it (`extern`) and
  C 9349's comment names where its definition lives (`From w32xfns.c`), so the port writes
  `unsafe extern "C" { pub static mut interrupt_handle: HANDLE; }` under `#[cfg (feature =
  "windowsnt")]` - a declaration, which is what the `missing file-scope object` row wanted
  (`--check` retires it).  The **definition** belongs to `src/w32xfns.rs`'s ledger when that unit
  lands (Owed 41), exactly the way slice 37 left `w32_pipe_buffer_size` to `src/w32proc.rs`.
- **A slice-40 fidelity correction rides along** (recorded here because it edits slice 40's text):
  C 9218's `min (waiting, (DWORD) count)` is `min (waiting, count as DWORD)` - the
  `include/lisp.rs` 3160 `min<T: PartialOrd>` mirror function, not `core::cmp::min` (both spell the
  token, but the mirror's own function is the faithful choice; the same applies to C 9540's
  `min (count, chunk)` in `sys_write`, which the next slice will meet).

### The forty-second slice's deviations (C 9351-9573)

- **Thirteen names are declared in-unit, all `UNVERIFIED`** (P-88, Owed 42):
  `<winbase.h>`'s `WriteFile`, `MsgWaitForMultipleObjects` and `WaitForMultipleObjects` (each with C's
  own parameter list), `PurgeComm`, `CancelIo`, `GetNamedPipeInfo`, the constants `INFINITE`
  (`0xFFFFFFFF`), `QS_ALLINPUT` (`0x04FF`), `PURGE_TXABORT` (`0x0001`) and `PURGE_TXCLEAR` (`0x0004`),
  the CRT's `_write (int, const void *, unsigned int)` and `_memccpy (void *, const void *, int,
  size_t)` (the `string.in.rs` 108 rename), and `keyboard.h`'s `detect_input_pending () -> bool` - which
  Owed 12's note had the lane check against `include/keyboard.rs` **before** declaring it (it was not
  there, so no `E0428` this time).  The macros/function the body leans on are mirrors':
  `USE_SAFE_ALLOCA`/`SAFE_NALLOCA`/`SAFE_FREE` (`include/lisp.rs` 3004/3059/3084, called with their
  `sa_avail`/`sa_count` arguments as `androidvfs.rs` 1573/1616 does) and `min` (lisp.rs 3160, slice 41's
  correction).
- **C 9445's `case WAIT_OBJECT_0 + 1:` needed a match guard.**  Rust rejects `WAIT_OBJECT_0 + 1` as a
  pattern (measured: the first frozen run failed on exactly that line), so the port writes
  `v if v == WAIT_OBJECT_0 + 1 =>` - the guard keeps C's arithmetic verbatim and binds the value; the
  first arm stays the plain `WAIT_OBJECT_0 =>` path C writes, and the trailing `_ => {}` covers no C
  case (C's `switch` would simply fall out).  This is recorded in the slice's `macro-body-notes.tsv` row
  together with `switch`/`case`.
- **C's pointer arithmetic and coercions keep their shapes**: the line loop's `next - dst` is
  `(next as isize - dst as isize) as c_int`, `src += copied` is `src.offset (copied as isize)`,
  `next[-1]`/`next[0]` are `*next.offset (-1)`/`*next.offset (0)`, `dst = next + 1` is
  `dst.offset (1)`, the chunk loop's `p += n`/`count -= n` are `p.offset (n as isize)`/`count -= n as
  c_uint`, the `(HANDLE)`/`(DWORD *)` casts are `as HANDLE`/`(&raw mut nchars) as *mut DWORD`, C 9534's
  two ternaries are one Rust `if … { … } else { … }` expression typed `c_uint`, the three `NULL`s of
  C 9527 are `std::ptr::null_mut ()`, C's one-line `if` (C 9459) stays one line, `interrupt_handle`
  (slice 41's extern) is read in C 9412's array literal, and `nchars`/`tmpbuf`/`dst`/`next`/
  `pipe_buffer_size` follow the C 7290 `h_snapshot` device while C's initial values (`src`, `dst`,
  `nbytes`, `p`, `is_pipe`, `chunk`) are kept as written.  The definition is exported.

### The forty-third slice's deviations (C 9574-9821)

- **The record chain is spelled in-unit** because the unit's `PIP_ADAPTER_INFO` is
  `pub type PIP_ADAPTER_INFO = *mut c_void;` (port line 494): `IP_ADDRESS_STRING`
  (`char String[4 * 4]`), `IP_ADDR_STRING` (`Next`/`IpAddress`/`IpMask`/`Context`) and
  `IP_ADAPTER_INFO` (C's eighteen members in C's order, `MAX_ADAPTER_NAME_LENGTH + 4`/
  `MAX_ADAPTER_DESCRIPTION_LENGTH + 4` arrays, the `UINT` fields as `c_uint`, the two `time_t`
  leases) - every one `UNVERIFIED` (P-88, Owed 43).  **All items of this slice carry
  `#[cfg (feature = "windowsnt")]`**; the first frozen run failed precisely because the records did
  not, which is the one revision the probe demanded of the declarations.
- **The constants and two calls are in-unit**: `AF_INET` (2), `ERROR_BUFFER_OVERFLOW` (111),
  `MIB_IF_TYPE_ETHERNET`/`_TOKENRING`/`_FDDI`/`_PPP`/`_LOOPBACK`/`_SLIP`
  (6/9/15/23/24/28), `IF_TYPE_IEEE80211` (71), `MAX_ADAPTER_NAME_LENGTH` (256),
  `MAX_ADAPTER_DESCRIPTION_LENGTH` (128), `MAX_ADAPTER_ADDRESS_LENGTH` (8), the `lisp.h` subr
  and `strstr` - the last of which the second arm run added (the `<string.h>` mirror
  has no `strstr` for this arm, so an in-unit declaration is the faithful reading of C 9637's call).
  `get_adapters_info` (port line 2917) is called with `ainfo as PIP_ADAPTER_INFO` and
  `&raw mut ainfo_len`.
- **C's `goto done` and its inline enum**: `'done: { … }` wraps C 9594-9816 and C 9606's
  `goto done` is `break 'done;`, so the block's tail is C 9818's label body (`xfree (ainfo);
  return res;`); C 9619-9629's inline `enum` is eight `const`s inside the loop body (P-57's family),
  C's two `switch`es are `match`es with C's labels (`|`-arms where C shares a body, `_` for C's
  `default:`), and C's `for (adapter = ainfo; adapter; adapter = adapter->Next)` is
  `adapter = ainfo; while ! adapter.is_null () { … }` with the increment written **before** each of
  C 9682-9683's and C 9691's two `continue`s (the slice-35 `for`+`continue` device).
- **C's `sizeof` and pointer coercions**: `sizeof (IP_ADAPTER_INFO)` is
  `core::mem::size_of::<IP_ADAPTER_INFO> ()`, `sizeof (struct sockaddr)` is
  `core::mem::size_of::<sockaddr> () as c_int` (the argument is an `int`), the `(struct sockaddr *)`
  casts are `&raw mut sa as *mut sockaddr`, C's `~net_mask` is `! net_mask`, the int-to-`short`
  stores are `AF_INET as c_short`, C's `xmalloc`/`xrealloc`/`xfree` take/return `*mut c_void` (so the
  record pointers are cast in and out), `sprintf`'s runtime format is
  `*ifmt.offset (ifmt_idx as isize)`, and `n < adapter->AddressLength` compares the `c_int` counter
  against the `c_uint` field written out (`as c_int`).  `nchars`-style locals (`if_num`, `ip_addr`,
  `ifmt_idx`, `n`, `net_mask`, `adapter`, `sa`) follow the C 7290 `h_snapshot` device, and the
  definition is exported (`#[unsafe(no_mangle)]`; C spells it `static`, which the port's own
  exported spelling is the lane's standing choice for such file-private definitions - recorded here
  as the slice's one shape decision).

### The forty-fourth slice's deviations (C 9822-9855)

- **Three names and one record chain are declared in-unit** (P-88, `UNVERIFIED`, Owed 44): `UINT8`
  (`typedef unsigned char UINT8;` - spelled as its own alias rather than the unit's `BYTE`, because C
  names it), `AF_INET6` (23), `in6_addr` (C's union of `u_char Byte[16]`/`u_short Word[8]`, kept as a
  `#[repr(C)]` union so its alignment matches C's) and `sockaddr_in6` (`short sin6_family; u_short
  sin6_port; u_long sin6_flowinfo; struct in6_addr sin6_addr; u_long sin6_scope_id;` - 28 bytes, C's
  member names and order).  Every item carries `#[cfg (feature = "windowsnt")]`.
- **A slice-43 correction rides along** (recorded here because it edits slice 43's text): C 9580
  spells `network_interface_get_info` **`static`**, so the port must be a file-private function - the
  lane's normal `unsafe fn`, as `socket_to_fd` (slice 25) and this slice's `address_prefix_match`
  are.  Slice 43 had written `#[unsafe(no_mangle)] pub unsafe extern "C" fn`, which would have
  exported a symbol C keeps private; the attribute and `pub` are removed and the corrected shape is
  what `--check` now reads (its `missing function` row does not change either way, which is why the
  slip survived slice 43's gates - the export set is what would have caught it had the name been in
  C's own `exported` list).
- **C's casts and its forty-bit arithmetic are written out**: the four address reads are
  `&raw mut (*(address as *mut sockaddr_in6)).sin6_addr as *mut UINT8` (and the `sockaddr_in`
  counterpart), C's `i < prefix_len >> 3` is `i < ((prefix_len >> 3) as c_int)` (the `ULONG` shift
  narrowed for the `int` counter), C 9845's array reads are `*address_data.offset (i as isize)`, and
  C 9851-9852's comparison is written with C's own promotion - both bytes as `c_int`, the mask
  `0xff << (8 - (prefix_len % 8) as c_int)` - so the `&` keeps C's integer widths.  `address_data`,
  `prefix_address_data` and `i` follow the C 7290 `h_snapshot` device.

### The forty-fifth slice's deviations (C 9856-10093)

- **The four records are spelled with only the members this file reads** - `SOCKET_ADDRESS`
  (`lpSockaddr`, `iSockaddrLength`), `IP_ADAPTER_UNICAST_ADDRESS` (`Next`, `Address`, `PrefixOrigin`,
  `SuffixOrigin`, `DadState`, `ValidLifetime`, `PreferredLifetime`, `LeaseLifetime`,
  `OnLinkPrefixLength`), `IP_ADAPTER_PREFIX` (`Next`, `Address`, `PrefixLength`) and
  `IP_ADAPTER_ADDRESSES` (`Next`, `IfType`, `Description`, `FirstUnicastAddress`, `FirstPrefix`) -
  in C's member order, each `#[repr(C)]` and `UNVERIFIED`.  **This is a recorded deviation, not a
  claim**: the SDK's real records carry members before, between and after these (the full lists are in
  Owed 45), so the offsets the port reads are only correct once that pass writes the complete records;
  the alternative (no spelling at all) would leave the function unportable, and the mirror's own
  deferral note (P-72 ③) is the reason the record chain is here rather than there.  The seven
  `IF_TYPE_*` values (6/9/15/23/24/28/131) and `AF_INET6` (slice 44) come from `<ipifcons.h>`.
- **C 10029's `#if _WIN32_WINNT >= 0x0600` is a new feature** (`win32_winnt_ge_0600`): the port writes
  the Vista arm (`address->OnLinkPrefixLength`) and the `#else` arm (`*(UINT8 *) (&address->
  LeaseLifetime + 1)`, spelled as the byte eight past `LeaseLifetime` - pointer arithmetic, not a
  member read, exactly as C writes it) as two `#[cfg]`-gated blocks.  The integrator registers the
  feature in `xtask`'s table and its `FLIPS` entry (Owed 45).
- **C's `match` parameter is `r#match`** (a Rust keyword; the file's `r#type`/`r#priv` device), and
  `DECLARE_POINTER_ALIAS (n, T, v)` is `let n: *mut T = v as *mut T;` (the macro's own definition:
  `type *n = (type *) (v)`); C's `continue`s inside the two `for` walks are spelled with the increment
  written first (the slice-35/43 device), C's `sizeof` calls are `core::mem::size_of::<T> () as c_int`,
  the two `->` chains that index a mask are `*maskp.offset (i as isize)`, C's `-1U` is `! 0u32`,
  `sys_htonl (-1U << (32 - numbits))` is written with C's `unsigned` shift (`(! 0u32) << (32 - numbits)
  as u32`), C's `(ifa_addr->sa_family == AF_INET6) ? 128 : 32` is a Rust `if … { … } else { … }`
  expression, and `ipv6`'s `#ifdef AF_INET6` guard is dropped because slice 44 declared `AF_INET6`
  unconditionally (recorded here).  The function's own `static`-less definition is exported, as C
  spells it.

### The forty-sixth slice's deviations (C 10094-10309)

- **C's inline `<winreg.h>` items are spelled in-unit.**  `KEY_READ` (`0x20019`) and the five
  `REG_*` values this file did not already carry (`REG_NONE` 0, `REG_BINARY` 3, `REG_DWORD` 4,
  `REG_MULTI_SZ` 7, `REG_QWORD` 11) are `pub const`s whose `/// C:` line carries C's own `#define`
  text; `RegOpenKeyEx`/`RegQueryValueEx`/`RegCloseKey` (C calls the unsuffixed names, which are the
  `…A` macros) are one `unsafe extern "system"` block, and `wcslen` / `from_unicode` one
  `unsafe extern "C"` each.  All are `UNVERIFIED` on this host (Owed 46).
- **The `switch` is a `match` and C's `break;` statements are dropped.**  C's seven labels keep C's
  order, `default:` is the `_` arm, and the seven `break;` lines (10196, 10199, 10202, 10213, 10233,
  10273, 10301) have no counterpart after a `match` arm; the arm set is recorded in the
  `macro-body-notes.tsv` row this slice adds.
- **The `REG_MULTI_SZ` arm carries an explicit brace pair.**  C's `case REG_MULTI_SZ:` body is two
  statements (`if (use_unicode) … else …`, then `retval = Fnreverse (val);`), which a Rust arm
  cannot hold without a block; that brace pair is the slice's only line pair with no C tick.
- **C's assignment-in-condition is a block expression.**  C 10138-142 and 10169-172's
  `if ((status = …) == … || (status = …) == …)` is
  `if ({ status = …; status }) == … as LONG || ({ status = …; status }) == … as LONG`, which keeps
  C's assignment order and the `||` short-circuit; C's `ERROR_NOT_SUPPORTED`/`ERROR_SUCCESS` are the
  `as LONG` conversions the same comparisons need.
- **The remaining spellings follow the lane's devices.**  C's two `do … while` loops are
  `loop { … if ! (…) { break; } }` (P-95); `L'\0'`/`L'/'`/`L'\\'` are the `0`/`0x2f`/`0x5c` stores
  they name and `wchar_t` is `c_ushort`, so the two arrays are `[c_ushort; …]` / `[c_char; …]` with
  `core::mem::size_of::<[…] > ()` for C's `sizeof`; the pointer walks C writes with `[]` are
  `.offset (… as isize)` reads/stores; `AUTO_STRING_WITH_LEN` is the mirror's `macro_rules!`
  (invoked with `!`); `is_windows_9x ()`'s `BOOL` is compared to C's `0` verbatim; and C 10112's
  `*key` stays uninitialised until C 10166 assigns it, as C has it.

### The forty-seventh slice's deviations (C 10310-10460)

- **MSVCRT's `<time.h>` and the CRT globals are spelled in-unit.**  `_dstbias` keeps C's `#ifndef`
  guard text as `/// C:` lines and becomes an extern static (the object row's retirement is the
  *name*, not a claim about the CRT); `_daylight` is its twin because C 10332 reads it while the
  reference declares only `_dstbias`; `clock_t`/`CLOCKS_PER_SEC` are MSVCRT's `long`/`1000` (the
  `src/timefns.rs` 92-93 pair is the *frozen target's* `c_ulong`/`1000000`, a different `<time.h>` -
  the two agree in width on every supported target); and C 10374's `clock` is the CRT's, for the
  reason the next entry gives.  All are `UNVERIFIED` (Owed 47).
- **C 75's `#undef clock` is why C 10374 is not recursion.**  The reference's `nt/inc/ms-w32.h` 247 is
  `#define clock sys_clock`, and `w32.c` includes that header at C 46; C 75 then `#undef`s the name, so
  `return clock ();` inside `sys_clock` calls **the CRT's** `clock`.  The port's `ms-w32.rs` 218 carries
  the macro as `macro_rules! clock`, which is not in this unit's scope, and this slice declares the CRT
  prototype (`clock_t clock (void);`) at its use site (P-88 ①).  Had the `#undef` been missed, the 1:1
  spelling would have been `return sys_clock ();` - an infinite recursion the arm probe cannot see (it
  stops at the resolution phase), which is why it is recorded here rather than left to the probe.
- **`LoadLibraryA` is declared beside the loader block's `LoadLibrary`.**  C 10428 names the A form
  literally while C 10414/C 10431 name the W forms; the loader block spells C's `LoadLibrary` macro
  target as `LoadLibrary` (its own note) and `GetModuleFileNameA` by its own name, so this slice adds
  `LoadLibraryA`/`LoadLibraryW`/`GetModuleFileNameW`/`DisableThreadLibraryCalls` - one CRT symbol
  reachable under two Rust names, exactly as C's macro pair has it.
- **`make_specified_string` is this unit's prototype.**  `src/lisp.h` 4566-4568 declares it, the
  `include/lisp.rs` mirror does not carry it, and `src/pgtkfns.rs` 617 spells the same prototype for
  its own unit - so this slice follows that shape.
- **The remaining spellings follow the lane's devices.**  The `LARGE_INTEGER` locals C writes as
  `user_int.LowPart` are `.u.LowPart` here (the record is a Rust union with a named `u` member, the
  `src/w32.rs` 373-381 note); `create`/`exit`/`kernel`/`user` and the three `LARGE_INTEGER` locals are
  zero-initialised where C leaves them uninitialised (PORTING.md 1.5); `sizeof (name_w)`/`sizeof
  (name_a)` are `core::mem::size_of_val (&name_w)` (a place, not a type); C 10400/10401's un-braced
  `for` is a braced block whose tail is a `while` with C's increment duplicated at the end of the body;
  C's `-1` store into `DWORD res` is `! 0u32`; the `(res > 0) ? ... : ...` expression is `if res > 0
  { ... } else { ... }` with C 10442's own comment carried inside it; and `get_process_times_fn`'s
  `if (x)` / `(*x) (...)` pair is the `is_some ()` / `.unwrap () (...)` form C 592/596 already set.

### The forty-eighth slice's deviations (C 10461-10562)

- **C's three `alloca` sites are the stack pool and its eight adjacent literals are `concat!`.**
  C 10494-10496's message buffer, C 10514's `msg_w` and C 10522's `msg_a` are `sa_stack_alloc (…)`
  (PORTING.md P-05), and C 10500-10509's eight adjacent literals are one `concat!` call whose
  arguments keep C's own text (including `\"` and `\n`), which is why the C body's `alloca` token
  cannot reappear - the notes row records it.
- **`w32.c` calls two names it never declares.**  C 10550's `remove_w32_kbdhook` has C 10544's own
  `extern void remove_w32_kbdhook (void);` (the comment above it says `from w32fns.c`) and C 10561's
  `w32_gdiplus_shutdown` has only `src/w32gui.h` 51, a header `w32.c` does not include.  The
  `include/w32term.rs` and `include/w32gui.rs` mirrors carry both prototypes but are **not** in this
  unit's include graph, so the arm probe reads both as unresolved and this slice declares them
  in-unit (P-88 ①) - the slice-31 rule is about names the unit's *own* graph declares, and these
  are not.
- **`abort` is also this unit's.**  `lib/include/stdlib.in.rs` 176 carries `fn abort`, but the
  include graph reaches `lib/include/stdlib.rs` (C 29's `#include <stdlib.h>`) and never that
  template, so the prototype is spelled here.
- **`MessageBox` keeps C's spelling of the macro target.**  C 10529 writes `MessageBox` (the
  `…A`/`…W` alias under `_UNICODE`), exactly as the loader block keeps `LoadLibrary`; the `/// C:`
  line carries `MessageBoxA`'s prototype, and `HWND`/`MB_OK`/`MB_ICONEXCLAMATION`/`MB_TASKMODAL` come
  with it (`include/w32.rs` 27's `void *` reading for the handle).
- **The remaining spellings follow the lane's devices.**  `!noninteractive`/`!inhibit_window_system`
  are the `bool` statics' negations and C 10548's `(void) ignored;` is `let _ = ignored;`; C's two
  deferred locals (`init_file`, `fd`) are declared without initialisers (PORTING.md 1.5) and
  assigned where C assigns them; the two `NULL`/`0` groups in C 10485-10487's `openp` call are
  `std::ptr::null_mut ()` and `false`; and the two loaded-pointer calls are the `.unwrap () (…)`
  form with `CP_UTF8 as UINT` / `CP_ACP as UINT` and `multiByteToWideCharFlags as c_ulong` (the
  C 1768/1795 reading).

### The forty-ninth slice's deviations (C 10563-10686)

- **C 10664's `signal` is `sys_signal`, and the port spells the renamed target.**  `w32.c` includes
  `nt/inc/ms-w32.h` at C 46 (whose 252 is `#define signal sys_signal`) and its C 51-75 `#undef` block
  cancels `access`…`clock` but **not** `signal`, so C 10664 preprocesses to
  `sys_signal (SIGABRT, term_ntproc)`.  `sys_signal`'s prototype is the in-graph `ms-w32.rs` 318's
  (`signal_handler` = `Option<unsafe extern "C" fn (c_int)>`, ms-w32.rs 133), so the handler is
  `Some (term_ntproc)` - the lane's standing check 8, and the reason the `signal` token needed a
  notes row.
- **`SIGABRT` is the in-graph mirror's value, not the arm's.**  `lib/include/signal.rs` 7 (which
  `w32.c`'s C 36 `<signal.h>` reaches) carries the **frozen target's** measured `6`; MinGW-w64's
  `<signal.h>` for this arm reads **22** (MSVCRT's `_SIGABRT`).  Re-spelling it in-unit would be an
  `E0428` against that mirror, so this slice uses the mirror's value and records the difference here
  and in Owed 49 - a Windows-host pass must settle it.
- **The `_UCRT` pair is carried as C's own predicate.**  C 10587's `#ifdef _UCRT` is
  `#[cfg (feature = "ucrt")]` and C 10600's `#else` its `not (…)` twin (both arms inside the
  `windowsnt`-gated function, so no `all (…)` is needed), the feature the `nt/cmdproxy.rs` 49
  precedent registered.
- **Three more CRT/SDK names are this unit's.**  `GetStdHandle` (with the three `STD_*_HANDLE`
  arguments C 10594-10599 passes, `-10`/`-11`/`-12` as `DWORD`), `SetHandleInformation` (the socket
  layer's `pfn_SetHandleInformation` at `src/w32.rs` 11231 is a *different* name, loaded through
  `LOAD_PROC`, so it does not supply this call) and `_open_osfhandle` (the CRT's `<io.h>`), none of
  which any mirror in this unit's graph declares.
- **The remaining spellings follow the lane's devices.**  C 10671's `char drive[] = "A:\\"` is the
  four-byte array C's initialiser writes (`'A'`, `':'`, `'\\'`, NUL) because C 10680 increments it in
  place, so its reads/writes are `*drive.as_ptr ()`/`*drive.as_mut_ptr ()`; `!dumping` is
  `dumping == 0` and C 10580's `getenv (…) != NULL` is `! (…).is_null ()`; `DRIVE_INDEX (…)` is the
  `DRIVE_INDEX!` macro with C's `c_int` argument cast to `c_int` and the index to `usize`;
  `(GetDriveType (…) == DRIVE_FIXED)` stores with C's `as BOOL` (the C 580 reading); and the four
  `static mut` items this body writes (`winsock_lib`, `stdin_save`-style locals, `fixed_drives`,
  `volume_cache`) keep C's own `NULL`/`INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE` initialisers.

### The fiftieth slice's deviations (C 10687-10732)

- **C 10693's declaration is the object, and the port spells it as an extern static.**  C's text is
  `extern DWORD dwMainThreadId;` (the definition lives in `w32fns.c`/`w32term.c`, whose
  `include/w32term.rs` mirror carries only C's *comment* about the pair, not the declaration), so the
  name is spelled here - which is what retires the lane's last `missing file-scope object` row.
- **The three `CTRL_*_EVENT` values and `GetCurrentThreadId` are in-unit.**  No mirror in this unit's
  graph declares either group; the values are `<wincon.h>`'s 2/5/6 and the function is the SDK's
  `DWORD WINAPI GetCurrentThreadId (void)` (`<processthreadsapi.h>`).  `UNVERIFIED` (Owed 50).
- **`clear_message_stack` and `shut_down_emacs` are cross-TU declarations this unit spells.**
  `src/lisp.h` 4381 and 5145 declare them, `src/xdisp.rs` and `src/emacs.rs` define them, and
  `include/lisp.rs` carries neither - so the two prototypes are here (`src/android.rs` 2431 spells
  the same `shut_down_emacs` for its own unit).
- **C's `static BOOL WINAPI` is a non-`pub` `unsafe extern "system" fn`.**  The lane's standing
  check 5: a C `static` definition stays unexported, so this one carries no `#[unsafe(no_mangle)]`
  (unlike the slice's neighbours `init_ntproc`/`term_ntproc`, which C declares without `static`).
- **The remaining spellings follow the lane's devices.**  C's `type` parameter is `r#type` (the
  reserved-keyword device the file already uses for `match`/`priv`); C 10710's `if (noninteractive)`
  is the `bool` static's plain test; `Vquit_flag = Qkill_emacs;`/`Vinhibit_quit = Qnil;` keep C's
  two assignments (`Qkill_emacs ()`/`Qnil ()` being the symbol accessors); C 10711's un-braced body
  gets its brace pair; and the extern static read at C 10703 is C's own comparison.

### The fifty-first slice's deviations (C 10733-10827)

- **The two charset entry points are declared with the mirror's own `*_Proc` signature.**  C 10783-10784
  takes their addresses (`pMultiByteToWideChar = MultiByteToWideChar;`) while C 10750-10753 loads the
  same names through `get_proc_addr` on the 9X arm.  `MultiByteToWideChar_Proc`/`WideCharToMultiByte_Proc`
  (`include/w32.rs` 270-275) are `Option<unsafe extern "system" fn (c_uint, c_ulong, …)>`, so the two
  bare declarations repeat that spell (raw `c_uint`/`c_ulong`/`u16` rather than the SDK aliases) and
  `Some (…)` coerces - the alternative (the alias spelling) would need a cast at the assignment, which
  C does not have.
- **C's two casts are the file's `transmute` device.**  C 10750-10753's `(MultiByteToWideChar_Proc)
  get_proc_addr (…)` is a C function-pointer cast; the port writes the
  `std::mem::transmute::<VOIDFNPTR, …>` form C 635-636, 663-666, 697-700, … already set for the same
  loader.  Recorded because the frozen probe caught the literal transcription.
- **`switch (button)` with C's two shared labels is one `match` arm.**  C 10772-10773's `case IDOK:`
  and `default:` have one body, so the port writes `IDOK | _ => { exit (1); }` (C's label order) -
  which is why the token line needed a notes row.
- **`MB_ERR_INVALID_CHARS` is a `DWORD` here.**  C stores it into `multiByteToWideCharFlags`
  (C's `DWORD`, `src/w32.rs` 1209), where the other three `MB_*` values are C's `MessageBox` `UINT`
  argument - the lane's standing check 2.
- **The remaining spellings follow the lane's devices.**  `if (ret)` is `! ret.is_null ()` and
  `GetModuleFileNameA (NULL, …)` is `std::ptr::null_mut ()` with C's truth test as `!= 0`;
  `LoadLibrary ("Unicows.dll")` keeps the loader block's A-form spelling with `c"…".as_ptr ()`;
  C's adjacent literals are one `concat!`; `_mbsrchr (…, '\\')` passes `b'\\' as c_uint` (the
  mirror's `c_uint` parameter) and `*p = '\0'` is `*p = 0`; `epath_dir + 11` is
  `epath_dir.offset (11)`; C 10818's assignment-in-condition is the `{ …; p }` block expression the
  file already uses; and `w32_relocate`'s parameter is `mut` because C reassigns it.

### The fifty-second slice's deviations (C 10829-10858)

- **C 10860-10897's `realpath` cannot be defined until a mirror is re-gated.**  `include/w32.rs` 458
  declares `realpath` under `#[cfg (feature = "windowsnt")]` (C 195-197's block, whose comment says
  "the name is also declared by the port's system mirrors, hence the feature"), so a definition in
  `femacs/src/w32.rs` would collide with it in the arm configuration - one `E0428` *and* a
  `check-mirror-dupes.py` finding, which is why this slice stops at C 10858.  The fix is the one Owed
  16 already used for `sys_stat.in.rs`'s five names: re-gate that declaration to
  `#[cfg (not (feature = "windowsnt"))]`, since on the arm `w32.c` **is** the definition and on every
  other arm `w32.c` is not compiled at all.  Recorded as Owed 52 with the measured line number.
- **The function-local `static` keeps C's own initialiser.**  C 10838's `static char exename[MAX_PATH]`
  is a `static mut exename: [c_char; MAX_PATH as usize] = [0; MAX_PATH as usize]` inside the body (the
  `s_b_ret`/`s_pfn_*` device this file already uses), so its reads and writes are `exename.as_ptr ()`
  and `exename.as_mut_ptr ()`.
- **C's pointer-identity test is pointer identity here.**  C 10853's `if (tgt != exename_utf8)`
  compares `chase_symlinks`' return against the array's own address (the callee returns its argument
  when it is not a symlink), so the port writes `tgt != exename_utf8.as_ptr ()` - not a `strcmp`, which
  would answer a different question.
- **C 10839's negation is `== 0`.**  `if (!GetModuleFileNameA (NULL, exename, MAX_PATH))` is the
  `== 0` form with C's `NULL` as `std::ptr::null_mut ()`, and the early `return NULL;` is
  `return std::ptr::null_mut ();`.
- **C 10844's truth test is `!= 0`.**  `get_volume_info (…)`'s `int` result is compared against `0`
  (the lane's device) while the `&` mask test keeps C's own parenthesised shape.

**Correction to the fifty-second slice's deviations.**  Its `realpath` bullet is wrong on both counts,
and the fifty-third slice's arm reading is the evidence: `tools/check-mirror-dupes.py` reads bodyless
declarations only (a `fn` with a body is a definition, and defining what a glob-imported mirror
declares is the intended shape), and no `E0428` follows either - `include/w32.rs` 458's declaration
lives in a module, so the unit's top-level definition shadows it.  The bullet's "cannot be defined" and
"must first be re-gated" are therefore struck; the mirror needs no change.  (The lesson that *is* true
- a name a mirror declares must not be re-*declared* by the unit - stays as standing check 1.)

### The fifty-third slice's deviations (C 10859-10938)

- **C's function-pointer casts are the file's `transmute` device, for the third slice running.**
  C 10916-10919 spells `s_pfn_… = (Some_Proc) get_proc_addr (…)`; Rust has no such cast, so the port
  writes `std::mem::transmute::<VOIDFNPTR, …> (get_proc_addr (…))` (C 635-636's device).  The frozen
  probe caught the literal transcription again, so the shape is named here: reach for the `transmute`
  form the moment a `get_proc_addr` result is stored through a `*_Proc` alias.
- **`realpath` is defined although a mirror declares the name.**  The definition is legal (see the
  correction above) and it is what retires the checklist row; the mirror is untouched, because its
  `feature = "windowsnt"` gate is what gives *other* units the declaration on that arm.
- **`get_console_font_size` keeps C's `static` linkage.**  C 10899's `static void` is a private
  `unsafe fn` with no `#[unsafe(no_mangle)]` (standing check 5) - the file's second such case after
  `shutdown_handler`.
- **C 10930-10931's nested call is one statement.**  `COORD font_size = s_pfn_Get_Console_Font_Size
  (hscreen, font_info.nFont);` spans two C lines here, and the two stores keep C's narrowing from
  `COORD`'s `c_short` (`as c_int`).
- **The remaining spellings follow the lane's devices.**  C's `!is_windows_9x ()` is
  `is_windows_9x () == 0`; the two `if (s_pfn_…)` truth tests are `.is_some ()` and the calls are
  `.unwrap () (…)`; C 10926's uninitialised `CONSOLE_FONT_INFO font_info;` is `core::mem::zeroed ()`
  (PORTING.md 1.5); `LoadLibrary ("Kernel32.dll")` keeps the loader block's A-form spelling; and
  `realpath`'s two array pairs are `[c_ushort; MAX_PATH as usize]` / `[c_char; MAX_PATH as usize]` with
  `MAX_PATH as DWORD` for C's `nBufferLength`.

### The fifty-fourth slice's deviations (C 10939-11014)

- **Four records are spelled in full, because the OS writes them.**  C 10949's `STARTUPINFO si;` and
  C 10951's `PROCESS_INFORMATION proc_info;` are filled by `GetStartupInfo`/`CreateProcess`, so the
  port spells the SDK's *complete* `STARTUPINFO` (all eighteen members, in C's order) rather than only
  the five fields this body reads - the same reasoning slice 45 could not use for `IP_ADAPTER_*`,
  where C read an OS-provided buffer through a pointer.  `SECURITY_ATTRIBUTES`, `SMALL_RECT` and
  `CONSOLE_SCREEN_BUFFER_INFO` come with them (the last two are the `src/w32console.rs` 174/204 shapes,
  the first the `nt/runemacs.rs` 178 shape).  All `UNVERIFIED` (Owed 54).
- **`chdir` is the CRT's, not `sys_chdir`.**  C 52's `#undef chdir` cancels `ms-w32.h`'s
  `#define chdir sys_chdir` (the C 51-75 block), so C 10994 preprocesses to the CRT's `chdir` - the
  in-graph `lib/include/unistd.in.rs` 57 declaration - and *not* to this file's own `sys_chdir
  (C 4403)`.  The lane's standing check 8 in the other direction: the `#undef` list is what decides.
- **Three locals C leaves uninitialised are zeroed.**  C 10949's `si`, C 10951's `proc_info` and
  C 10961's `screen_info` are filled by the entry points that take their addresses, so the port writes
  `core::mem::zeroed ()` (PORTING.md 1.5) - and C 10967's two `int`s, which `get_console_font_size`
  writes before reading, start at `0` for the same reason.
- **C's two implicit narrowing/widening pairs are spelled.**  `si.dwXCountChars =
  screen_info.dwSize.X;` widens `COORD`'s `c_short` into a `DWORD` (`as DWORD`), and the two
  `(srWindow.Right - srWindow.Left + 1) * font_width` products keep C's int arithmetic before the
  `as DWORD` store.
- **The remaining spellings follow the lane's devices.**  C 10996-11005's ten-argument call is
  one `CreateProcess` statement with `std::ptr::null ()`/`std::ptr::null_mut ()` for C's five `NULL`s
  and `&raw mut si`/`&raw mut proc_info` for its two address-of arguments; the two `if (… )` truth
  tests on `BOOL` results are `!= 0`; `exit (0)` is slice 51's in-unit prototype; and
  `errno = ENOEXEC;` is the file's `*errno () = ENOEXEC;` device.

### The fifty-fifth slice's deviations (C 11015-11143)

- **`CreateFile` is spelled beside the SDK section's `…A`/`…W` pair.**  C 11114 writes the macro's
  name; the unit already declares `CreateFileA`/`CreateFileW` (the SDK section's pair, spelled for an
  earlier slice), so this slice adds C's own name with the A form's signature - the loader-block
  reading the `LoadLibrary`/`GetModuleHandle` pair set (and `src/w32.rs` 404-409's note names).
- **`globals_of_w32`'s two vanished tokens are the port's own spellings.**  C 11094/11096's
  `w32_unicode_filenames = 0;` / `= 1;` are the `bool` static's `false`/`true`, and C 11102's
  `(HCRYPTPROV)0` is a plain `0` because `HCRYPTPROV` is `usize` (C's cast has no Rust counterpart
  for an integer alias) - both recorded in the `macro-body-notes.tsv` row this slice adds.
- **C 11098's `#ifdef HAVE_MODULES` is the `have_modules` feature.**  The declaration C 91-93
  produced (`all (feature = "windowsnt", feature = "have_modules")`, `src/w32.rs` 185-190) is what
  the call site needs, so the one-line body is gated with the same feature rather than the whole
  function.
- **`serial_open`'s four error calls keep C's varargs shape.**  `error` is `include/lisp.rs` 3824's
  varargs `!`-returning declaration, so C's `error ("Could not open %s", port)` is the
  `error (c"…".as_ptr (), port)` call the registry slice already used; C 11124/11137/11140's
  no-argument forms pass nothing after the format.
- **The remaining spellings follow the lane's devices.**  `new_child ()` returns `*mut c_void`
  (`include/w32.rs` 379), so C's `child_process *cp = new_child ();` is the `as *mut child_process`
  cast; `!cp` and `== NULL` tests are `.is_null ()`; `dflt_group_name`'s array decay is
  `&raw mut dflt_group_name as *mut c_char` (the `src/w32.rs` 3863 idiom); `hnd as intptr_t` is
  C's `(intptr_t) hnd`; and `fd_info[fd]`'s four accesses cast the `c_int` index with `as usize`.

### The fifty-sixth slice's deviations (C 11144-11315)

- **`DCB`'s fourteen bitfields are one `bits` word, and the masks carry C's own names.**  PORTING.md
  1.3's device (`Lisp_Marker`'s `bits`, `itree_node`'s) applied to an SDK record: `fBinary` …
  `fAbortOnError` become the `bits` word's masks, LSB first, and they are named after C's members so
  each write still spells the token C's body does (`dcb.bits |= fBinary;` for C 11177's
  `dcb.fBinary = TRUE;`).  The two 2-bit fields need their mask as well as their value; only
  `fRtsControl` is assigned a non-zero value (C 11278's `RTS_CONTROL_HANDSHAKE`), so only its shift is
  a constant.  The packing is asserted by `#[repr(C)]` order, not measured - `UNVERIFIED` (Owed 56).
- **The `TRUE`/`FALSE` tokens are the price of that device.**  Every `TRUE`/`FALSE` in C's body sits in
  a bitfield assignment, so the port's mask writes lose both tokens; the `macro-body-notes.tsv` row
  says so, and every other token of the function is still compared.
- **`plist_member`/`plist_get`/`plist_put` are this unit's declarations.**  `src/lisp.h` declares the
  three; no mirror in this unit's graph carries any of them (`include/lisp.rs` names them in comments
  only), and `src/process.rs` 1878/2082 spells two of them for its own unit - so this slice declares
  all three, which is what closed the six unresolved call sites.
- **`sizeof (DCB)` is a type and `sizeof (dcb)` a place.**  C 11173's `memset (&dcb, 0, sizeof (dcb))`
  keeps `core::mem::size_of::<DCB> ()` (C's argument is the *type* here) while C 11174's
  `dcb.DCBlength = sizeof (DCB);` is the same expression narrowed by `as DWORD`.
- **The remaining spellings follow the lane's devices.**  C's `summary[4] = "???"` is the four-byte
  array C's initialiser writes (C 11205/11248 increment into it with `+ '0'`, spelled
  `(XFIXNUM (tem) + b'0' as EMACS_INT) as c_char`); C 11152/11153's uninitialised `dcb`/`ct` are
  `core::mem::zeroed ()` (PORTING.md 1.5); the `!`-negated API tests are `== 0`; the six plist
  lookups keep C's if/else shape; and `fd_info[( *p).outfd as usize]`/`fd_info[infd as usize]` cast
  the `c_int` indices.

## Owed by the integrator (nothing here is claimed as done)

1. `tools/check-port-lines.py --write-pending`: **no action needed any more** - the run reports
   `0 file(s) under ten percent, 0 reference line(s); 0 ratchet row(s)`, because the port's ratio
   (6,332 / 11,362 = 55.7%) is above the threshold and the previous slice's stale row is gone.
2. `docs/ports/line-drift-tolerance.tsv`: one row, `w32`, tolerance >= 0.52, basis `total`, with
   the slice's reason from the Deviations.
3. `femacs/xtask/Cargo.toml`: the four features the previous slice named (`unicode`,
   `win32_winnt_lt_0501`, `mingw32_w32api_ge_5_2`, `mingw32_w32api_lt_3_15`) **plus one new one**:
   `win32_winnt_ge_0501` (C 2471's `#if _WIN32_WINNT >= 0x0501`, named after C's own predicate -
   the `w32fns.rs` C 167 `win32_windows_ge_0410` reading).
4. `docs/ports/excluded-macros.txt`: the two rows the previous slice owed (`DEFER_MS_W32_H`,
   `ORIG_WIN32_WINNT`) are unchanged; this slice adds none.
5. `docs/ports/token-aliases.tsv`: the previous slice's two rows, **plus** the families behind this
   slice's 14 token lines - `switch`/`case`/`break`/`default` -> `match` and its arms,
   `FALLTHROUGH` -> the repeated body, `alloca` -> `sa_stack_alloc` (P-05), `double` -> `f64`,
   `asm volatile ("" ::: "memory")` -> `core::sync::atomic::compiler_fence`.
6. `femacs/src/include/w32.rs`: **twenty-one** names, not four - the scan reports `E0428` for

    port spells the *renamed* target, so the arm probe can read a mirror's `macro_rules!` name without a
    duplicate (47); and a mirror that carries a prototype is only proof it is in scope when the
    unit's own `include!` graph reaches it - `include/w32term.rs`'s `remove_w32_kbdhook` and

    already *declares* at the arm's own value is used as-is (49 - `signal`→`sys_signal` spelled,
    `SIGABRT` taken from the mirror); and a C declaration with external linkage is an extern static
    whose definition lives in another unit, while the same C line under `static` would be a private
    item (50 - `dwMainThreadId` spelled, `shutdown_handler` left unexported); and a name whose
    *address* C takes must be declared with the exact signature of the `Option<unsafe extern … fn>`

    module-scoped declaration is *shadowed* by the unit's definition - but it may not be
    re-*declared* (52, corrected in 53 by `realpath`'s arm reading).

### Not claimed

Every line of both slices is behind a gate the frozen configuration leaves false: the `build-tu`
readings are an empty object (336 bytes) and the probe's are compile-level.  **Nothing here was
executed** - no `LoadLibrary`, no `CreateSymbolicLink`, no `GetCPInfo`, no registry, socket, volume
or directory call.  There is **no Windows-target compile and no layout measurement**: the arm flip
is `rustc` on this machine with `--cfg 'feature="windowsnt"'`, every SDK record is `UNVERIFIED`, and
the arm reading is **blocked by the mirror graph** (**71 error lines / 74 located sites** in the
`xtask probe` after slice 46, and the run stops at the resolution phase, so no type-level reading of
the new text exists), of which **five of the remaining eight sites are this unit's own
text**: the three SDK rows slice 15 reached (`REPARSE_DATA_BUFFER` twice and
`FSCTL_GET_REPARSE_POINT` once - Owed 18) and `DebPrint` (twice, since C 8170 is its second
site in this file), the other three sites being mirror names (`_MAX_PATH`, `O_NOINHERIT`,
`ws_fd_set`); `ws_fd_set` (one row) is still the one including-unit
socket name the mirror leaves to a reader.  Slices 13-15's own `E0428`s were discharged by the three mirror gates (Owed 16, 17, 19);
slice 14's `is_symlink` retired six unresolved sites and this slice's `chase_symlinks` the last
two of that name.  The file's `#if` verdicts are
`tools/check-cond-eval.py`'s (itself `[NOT VERIFIED]` for this file), not clang's.  C 11316-11362 is
**not ported here**: the 2 missing functions, 0 objects, 0 macros and 0 constants of the
`--check` reading are that boundary, not defects (`missing constant` is empty and the macro list
emptied with `SOCK_HANDLE`; the token lines slices 21-22 produced are recorded in their
`macro-body-notes.tsv` rows, and Evidence ㊺-㊿ and ➀-⓮ list the readings).  The
`win32_winnt_ge_0501` and `wsaenomore` features select nothing on this machine (the eleven
`WSAENOMORE`-arm values, the forty plain-block values, the CRT's two names, `INVALID_SOCKET` and
this slice's four records and four names, `SOCKET_ERROR`, `INADDR_NONE` and
`MAX_COMPUTERNAME_LENGTH` and `NO_RECOVERY` are `UNVERIFIED`, Owed 24-31), none of
`sys_strerror`, `sys_socket`, `socket_to_fd`, `sys_bind`, `sys_connect`, the five byte-order
wrappers, `sys_gethostname`, `sys_gethostbyname`, `sys_getservbyname` or `sys_getpeername` was called (no
socket was created, bound, connected, duplicated or allocated; no address was converted, no host
name or service record was resolved), and the token lines are checker readings, not behaviour
claims.
